+use serde_json::Value;
+use std::{
+    path::{Path, PathBuf},
+    process::Command,

+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);

+#[test]
+fn final_scan_distinguishes_tls_versions_and_rejects_malformed_go_retract() {
+    let f = Fixture(std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("pqc-semantic-cli-{}", std::process::id())));
+    let input = f.0.join("input");
+    std::fs::create_dir_all(&input).unwrap();
+    for version in [
+        "TLSv1_0", "TLSv1_1", "TLSv1.0", "TLSv1.1", "TLSv1_2", "TLSv1_3", "TLSv1.2", "TLSv1.3",
+    ] {
+        std::fs::write(
+            input.join(format!("{version}.js")),
+            format!("const version = \"{version}\";\n"),
+        )
+        .unwrap();
+    }
+    std::fs::write(
+        input.join("go.mod"),
+        "module example.org/app\nrequire example.org/lib v1.2.3\nretract banana\n",
+    )
+    .unwrap();
+    let output = f.0.join("output");
+    let r = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_pqc-scan"))
+        .arg("scan")
+        .arg(&input)
+        .args([
+            "--format",
+            "all",
+            "--diagnostics",
+            "--inventory-json",
+            "--rules-dir",
+        ])
+        .arg(Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../rules"))
+        .arg("--out-dir")
+        .arg(&output)
+        .output()
+        .unwrap();
+    assert!(r.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&r.stderr));
+    let report: Value =
+        serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(output.join("report.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
+    let hits = report["findings"].as_array().unwrap();
+    for version in ["TLSv1_0", "TLSv1_1", "TLSv1.0", "TLSv1.1"] {
+        assert!(
+            hits.iter()
+                .any(|h| h["rule_id"] == "TS_TLS_LEGACY_VERSION_LITERAL"
+                    && h["location"]["file"] == format!("{version}.js")),
+            "{version}"
+        );
+    }
+    assert!(hits.iter().all(|h| !matches!(
+        h["location"]["file"].as_str(),
+        Some("TLSv1_2.js" | "TLSv1_3.js" | "TLSv1.2.js" | "TLSv1.3.js")
+    )));
+    let diagnostics: Value =
+        serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(output.join("diagnostics.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(diagnostics["coverage"]["partial_files"], 1);
+    assert!(diagnostics["diagnostics"]
+        .as_array()
+        .unwrap()
+        .iter()
+        .any(|d| d["file"] == "go.mod"
+            && d["code"] == "detector_partial"
+            && d["detector"] == "dependency_detector"));
+}

+#[test]
+fn a123_repairs_reach_all_reports_and_keep_stable_findings_and_invalid_controls() {
+    let f = Fixture(std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("pqc-a123-cli-{}", std::process::id())));
+    let input = f.0.join("input");
+    std::fs::create_dir_all(&input).unwrap();
+    for (name, text) in [
+        ("types.ts", "type Shape<> = { v: true };\ntype View = () => import('view').Node[];\nconst alg = 'RS256';"),
+        ("view.tsx", "const view = <A href='/start?first-key=1&second-key=2' other={x && y} />;\nconst alg = 'RS256';"),
+        ("Dockerfile.java", "FROM base\nENV TOKEN_ALG RS256\n"),
+        ("Gemfile.lock", "GEM\n  remote: https://rubygems.org/\n  specs:\n    openssl (3.3.0-arm64-darwin)\n\nPLATFORMS\n  arm64-darwin\n\nBUNDLED WITH\n  4.0.12\n"),
+    ] { std::fs::write(input.join(name), text).unwrap(); }
+    let mut expected_ids = None;
+    for pass in 0..3 {
+        if pass == 2 {
+            std::fs::write(input.join("invalid.ts"), "type Shape<> = ;").unwrap();
+        }
+        let output = f.0.join(format!("output-{pass}"));
+        let r = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_pqc-scan"))
+            .arg("scan")
+            .arg(&input)
+            .args([
+                "--format",
+                "all",
+                "--diagnostics",
+                "--inventory-json",
+                "--cyclonedx-17",
+                "--repository-scope",
+                "a123",
+                "--rules-dir",
+            ])
+            .arg(Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../rules"))
+            .arg("--out-dir")
+            .arg(&output)
+            .output()
+            .unwrap();
+        assert!(r.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&r.stderr));
+        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_dir(&output).unwrap().count(), 10);
+        let read = |name| {
+            serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&std::fs::read(output.join(name)).unwrap()).unwrap()
+        };
+        let diagnostics = read("diagnostics.json");
+        assert_eq!(
+            diagnostics["coverage"]["partial_files"],
+            if pass == 2 { 1 } else { 0 }
+        );
+        assert_eq!(diagnostics["coverage"]["analysis_failed_files"], 0);
+        let report = read("report.json");
+        let hits = report["findings"].as_array().unwrap();
+        for name in ["types.ts", "view.tsx", "Dockerfile.java"] {
+            assert!(
+                hits.iter()
+                    .any(|h| h["location"]["file"] == name && h["rule_id"] == "JWT_RS256"),
+                "{name}"
+            );
+        }
+        assert_eq!(
+            read("report.sarif")["runs"][0]["results"]
+                .as_array()
+                .unwrap()
+                .len(),
+            hits.len()
+        );
+        let native = read("scan.inventory.v1.json");
+        let gem = native["assets"]
+            .as_array()
+            .unwrap()
+            .iter()
+            .find(|a| a["attributes"]["name"] == "openssl")
+            .unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(gem["attributes"]["reported_version"], "3.3.0-arm64-darwin");
+        assert_eq!(gem["attributes"]["resolved_version"], "3.3.0");
+        let mut ids = hits
+            .iter()
+            .map(|h| h["finding_id"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned())
+            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
+        ids.sort();
+        if let Some(expected) = &expected_ids {
+            assert_eq!(&ids, expected);
+        } else {
+            expected_ids = Some(ids);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn malformed_source_retains_findings_without_claiming_completed_analysis() {
+    let f =
+        Fixture(std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("pqc-partial-example-{}", std::process::id())));
+    let input = f.0.join("input");
+    std::fs::create_dir_all(&input).unwrap();
+    std::fs::write(
+        input.join("example.js"),
+        "const algorithm = 'RS256';\nfunction broken( {\n",
+    )
+    .unwrap();
+    let mut saved = None;
+    for valid in [false, true] {
+        if valid {
+            std::fs::write(
+                input.join("example.js"),
+                "const algorithm = 'RS256';\nfunction valid() {}\n",
+            )
+            .unwrap();
+        }
+        let output = f.0.join(if valid { "valid" } else { "broken" });
+        let r = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_pqc-scan"))
+            .arg("scan")
+            .arg(&input)
+            .args([
+                "--format",
+                "all",
+                "--diagnostics",
+                "--inventory-json",
+                "--cyclonedx-17",
+                "--rules-dir",
+            ])
+            .arg(Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../rules"))
+            .arg("--out-dir")
+            .arg(&output)
+            .output()
+            .unwrap();
+        assert!(r.status.success());
+        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_dir(&output).unwrap().count(), 10);
+        let d: Value =
+            serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(output.join("diagnostics.json")).unwrap())
+                .unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(d["coverage"]["read_ok_files"], 1);
+        assert_eq!(d["coverage"]["partial_files"], if valid { 0 } else { 1 });
+        let n: Value =
+            serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(output.join("report.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
+        let hit = n["findings"]
+            .as_array()
+            .unwrap()
+            .iter()
+            .find(|f| f["rule_id"] == "JWT_RS256")
+            .unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(hit["location"]["line"], 1);
+        if let Some(id) = &saved {
+            assert_eq!(&hit["finding_id"], id);
+        } else {
+            saved = Some(hit["finding_id"].clone());
+        }
+    }
+}

+//! Declared pre-inspection policy; cryptographic containers are never lumped in
+//! with media/archives. The opt-out permits explicit forensic/custom-detector use.
+use std::path::Path;
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default)]
+pub struct FileSelection {
+    pub include_non_scannable: bool,
+}
+pub(crate) const POLICY_LABEL: &str = "known non-scannable media, archives and binary data";
+pub(crate) fn known_extension(path: &Path) -> bool {
+    let ext = path
+        .extension()
+        .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
+        .unwrap_or("")
+        .to_ascii_lowercase();
+    matches!(
+        ext.as_str(),
+        "png"
+            | "jpg"
+            | "jpeg"
+            | "gif"
+            | "bmp"
+            | "ico"
+            | "webp"
+            | "tif"
+            | "tiff"
+            | "heic"
+            | "heif"
+            | "avif"
+            | "jxl"
+            | "jp2"
+            | "icns"
+            | "psd"
+            | "dds"
+            | "cur"
+            | "tga"
+            | "exr"
+            | "ktx"
+            | "j2c"
+            | "pfm"
+            | "glb"
+            | "mp3"
+            | "mp4"
+            | "wav"
+            | "webm"
+            | "mov"
+            | "avi"
+            | "ogg"
+            | "oga"
+            | "flac"
+            | "woff"
+            | "woff2"
+            | "ttf"
+            | "otf"
+            | "eot"
+            | "zip"
+            | "gz"
+            | "tgz"
+            | "tar"
+            | "xz"
+            | "lzma"
+            | "bz2"
+            | "zst"
+            | "z"
+            | "br"
+            | "lz4"
+            | "7z"
+            | "rar"
+            | "jar"
+            | "war"
+            | "ear"
+            | "egg"
+            | "whl"
+            | "wasm"
+            | "exe"
+            | "dll"
+            | "so"
+            | "dylib"
+            | "o"
+            | "obj"
+            | "class"
+            | "pyc"
+            | "pyc-tpl"
+            | "mo"
+            | "pb"
+            | "binpb"
+            | "desc"
+            | "pdf"
+    )
+}
+pub(crate) fn protects_crypto(path: &Path) -> bool {
+    let ext = path
+        .extension()
+        .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
+        .unwrap_or("")
+        .to_ascii_lowercase();
+    matches!(
+        ext.as_str(),
+        "pem"
+            | "crt"
+            | "cer"
+            | "der"
+            | "p12"
+            | "pfx"
+            | "pkcs12"
+            | "jks"
+            | "jceks"
+            | "bcfks"
+            | "keystore"
+            | "truststore"
+            | "keytab"
+            | "kbx"
+            | "key"
+            | "pub"
+            | "asc"
+    )
+}
+pub(crate) fn non_text(path: &Path, bytes: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    !protects_crypto(path)
+        && !crate::text_encoding::has_wide_bom(bytes)
+        && crate::text_encoding::decode(bytes).is_none()
+}

+#[cfg(test)]
+mod wide_text_repairs {
+    use super::*;
+    #[test]
+    fn utf16_source_is_decoded_with_and_without_bom() {
+        let source = "const key = 'RS256';\n";
+        for bom in [true, false] {
+            for little in [true, false] {
+                let mut bytes = if bom {
+                    if little {
+                        vec![0xff, 0xfe]
+                    } else {
+                        vec![0xfe, 0xff]
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    vec![]
+                };
+                for c in source.encode_utf16() {
+                    bytes.extend(if little {
+                        c.to_le_bytes()
+                    } else {
+                        c.to_be_bytes()
+                    });
+                }
+                let file = ScannableFile::from_bytes("code.js".into(), bytes);
+                assert_eq!(file.text.as_deref(), Some(source));
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    #[test]
+    fn utf32_source_is_decoded_with_and_without_bom() {
+        let source = "const 日 = 'RS256';\n";
+        for bom in [true, false] {
+            for little in [true, false] {
+                let mut bytes = if bom {
+                    if little {
+                        vec![0xff, 0xfe, 0, 0]
+                    } else {
+                        vec![0, 0, 0xfe, 0xff]
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    vec![]
+                };
+                for c in source.chars() {
+                    bytes.extend(if little {
+                        (c as u32).to_le_bytes()
+                    } else {
+                        (c as u32).to_be_bytes()
+                    });
+                }
+                let file = ScannableFile::from_bytes("code.js".into(), bytes);
+                assert_eq!(file.text.as_deref(), Some(source));
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    #[test]
+    fn encrypted_containers_stay_opaque_even_with_unicode_bom() {
+        let f = ScannableFile::from_bytes("opaque.p12".into(), vec![0xff, 0xfe, b'a', 0]);
+        assert!(f.text.is_none());
+    }
+}

diff --git a/crates/pqc_scan_core/src/repository_context.rs b/crates/pqc_scan_core/src/repository_context.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3542618
--- /dev/null
+++ b/crates/pqc_scan_core/src/repository_context.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,62 @@
+//! Read-only references to files already selected by this scan's walker.
+use crate::ScannableFile;
+use std::{
+    collections::BTreeSet,
+    path::{Component, Path, PathBuf},
+};
+
+pub struct RepositoryContext {
+    root: PathBuf,
+    selected: BTreeSet<PathBuf>,
+    max_bytes: usize,
+}
+fn normalized(path: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
+    let mut out = PathBuf::new();
+    for part in std::path::absolute(path).ok()?.components() {
+        match part {
+            Component::ParentDir => {
+                if !out.pop() {
+                    return None;
+                }
+            }
+            Component::CurDir => {}
+            _ => out.push(part.as_os_str()),
+        }
+    }
+    Some(out)
+}
+impl RepositoryContext {
+    pub(crate) fn new(root: &Path, selected: &[PathBuf], max_bytes: usize) -> Option<Self> {
+        Some(Self {
+            root: normalized(root)?,
+            selected: selected.iter().filter_map(|p| normalized(p)).collect(),
+            max_bytes: max_bytes.min(2 * 1024 * 1024),
+        })
+    }
+    pub fn read_relative(&self, from: &Path, relative: &Path) -> Option<ScannableFile> {
+        if relative.is_absolute() || relative.components().count() > 128 {
+            return None;
+        }
+        let target = normalized(&normalized(from)?.parent()?.join(relative))?;
+        if !target.starts_with(&self.root) || !self.selected.contains(&target) {
+            return None;
+        }
+        let bytes = crate::safe_read::read(&self.root, &target, self.max_bytes).ok()?;
+        Some(ScannableFile::from_bytes(target, bytes))
+    }
+    pub fn sibling_files(&self, from: &Path, extension: &str) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
+        let Some(from) = normalized(from) else {
+            return vec![];
+        };
+        let Some(parent) = from.parent() else {
+            return vec![];
+        };
+        self.selected
+            .range(parent.to_path_buf()..)
+            .take_while(|p| p.starts_with(parent))
+            .filter(|p| p.parent() == Some(parent) && p.extension().is_some_and(|e| e == extension))
+            .take(129)
+            .cloned()
+            .collect()
+    }
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,141 @@
+//! Strict wide-Unicode decoding with conservative BOM-less recognition.
+fn utf16(bytes: &[u8], little: bool) -> Option<String> {
+    if !bytes.len().is_multiple_of(2) {
+        return None;
+    }
+    let units: Vec<_> = bytes
+        .chunks_exact(2)
+        .map(|b| {
+            if little {
+                u16::from_le_bytes([b[0], b[1]])
+            } else {
+                u16::from_be_bytes([b[0], b[1]])
+            }
+        })
+        .collect();
+    String::from_utf16(&units).ok()
+}
+fn utf32(bytes: &[u8], little: bool) -> Option<String> {
+    if !bytes.len().is_multiple_of(4) {
+        return None;
+    }
+    bytes
+        .chunks_exact(4)
+        .map(|b| {
+            char::from_u32(if little {
+                u32::from_le_bytes([b[0], b[1], b[2], b[3]])
+            } else {
+                u32::from_be_bytes([b[0], b[1], b[2], b[3]])
+            })
+        })
+        .collect()
+}
+pub(crate) fn has_wide_bom(bytes: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    bytes.starts_with(&[0xff, 0xfe])
+        || bytes.starts_with(&[0xfe, 0xff])
+        || bytes.starts_with(&[0, 0, 0xfe, 0xff])
+}
+fn plausible(text: &str) -> bool {
+    !text.is_empty()
+        && text
+            .chars()
+            .all(|c| !c.is_control() || matches!(c, '\n' | '\r' | '\t' | '\u{c}'))
+}
+pub(crate) fn decode(bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<String> {
+    if let Some(rest) = bytes.strip_prefix(&[0xff, 0xfe, 0, 0]) {
+        return utf32(rest, true);
+    }
+    if let Some(rest) = bytes.strip_prefix(&[0, 0, 0xfe, 0xff]) {
+        return utf32(rest, false);
+    }
+    if let Some(rest) = bytes.strip_prefix(&[0xff, 0xfe]) {
+        return utf16(rest, true);
+    }
+    if let Some(rest) = bytes.strip_prefix(&[0xfe, 0xff]) {
+        return utf16(rest, false);
+    }
+    if let Some(rest) = bytes.strip_prefix(&[0xef, 0xbb, 0xbf]) {
+        return String::from_utf8(rest.to_vec()).ok();
+    }
+    let sample = &bytes[..bytes.len().min(1024)];
+    if !sample.contains(&0) {
+        return Some(String::from_utf8_lossy(bytes).into_owned());
+    }
+    // Require a strong ASCII lane signal; arbitrary binary is never lossily decoded.
+    for little in [true, false] {
+        if bytes.len() >= 16 && bytes.len().is_multiple_of(4) {
+            let units = sample.chunks_exact(4);
+            let count = units.len();
+            let ascii = units
+                .filter(|b| {
+                    if little {
+                        b[1..] == [0, 0, 0] && b[0] > 0 && b[0] < 128
+                    } else {
+                        b[..3] == [0, 0, 0] && b[3] > 0 && b[3] < 128
+                    }
+                })
+                .count();
+            if ascii * 4 >= count * 3 {
+                if let Some(s) = utf32(bytes, little).filter(|s| plausible(s)) {
+                    return Some(s);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for little in [true, false] {
+        if bytes.len() >= 8 && bytes.len().is_multiple_of(2) {
+            let units = sample.chunks_exact(2);
+            let count = units.len();
+            let ascii = units
+                .filter(|b| {
+                    if little {
+                        b[1] == 0 && b[0] > 0 && b[0] < 128
+                    } else {
+                        b[0] == 0 && b[1] > 0 && b[1] < 128
+                    }
+                })
+                .count();
+            if ascii * 4 >= count * 3 {
+                if let Some(s) = utf16(bytes, little).filter(|s| plausible(s)) {
+                    return Some(s);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    None
+}
+
+/// Transient decoded-UTF-8 to original-file byte boundaries for native locations.
+/// Ordinary UTF-8 requires no map. No source contents are retained in the map.
+pub(crate) fn source_offsets(bytes: &[u8], text: &str) -> Option<Vec<(usize, usize)>> {
+    let (width, bom) =
+        if bytes.starts_with(&[0xff, 0xfe, 0, 0]) || bytes.starts_with(&[0, 0, 0xfe, 0xff]) {
+            (4, 4)
+        } else if bytes.starts_with(&[0xff, 0xfe]) || bytes.starts_with(&[0xfe, 0xff]) {
+            (2, 2)
+        } else if bytes.starts_with(&[0xef, 0xbb, 0xbf]) {
+            (1, 3)
+        } else if bytes[..bytes.len().min(1024)].contains(&0) {
+            if bytes.len() == text.encode_utf16().count() * 2 {
+                (2, 0)
+            } else if bytes.len() == text.chars().count() * 4 {
+                (4, 0)
+            } else {
+                return None;
+            }
+        } else {
+            return None;
+        };
+    let mut raw = bom;
+    let mut map = Vec::with_capacity(text.chars().count() + 1);
+    for (offset, c) in text.char_indices() {
+        map.push((offset, raw));
+        raw += match width {
+            2 => c.len_utf16() * 2,
+            4 => 4,
+            _ => c.len_utf8(),
+        };
+    }
+    map.push((text.len(), raw));
+    Some(map)
+}

+fn known_non_certificate_pem(label: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    matches!(
+        label,
+        b"PUBLIC KEY"
+            | b"RSA PUBLIC KEY"
+            | b"PRIVATE KEY"
+            | b"ENCRYPTED PRIVATE KEY"
+            | b"RSA PRIVATE KEY"
+            | b"EC PRIVATE KEY"
+            | b"DSA PRIVATE KEY"
+            | b"OPENSSH PRIVATE KEY"
+            | b"CERTIFICATE REQUEST"
+            | b"NEW CERTIFICATE REQUEST"
+            | b"X509 CRL"
+            | b"DH PARAMETERS"
+            | b"DSA PARAMETERS"
+            | b"EC PARAMETERS"
+    )
+}
+fn known_non_certificate_der(bytes: &[u8]) -> bool {
+    use x509_parser::asn1_rs::{Any, FromDer, Tag};
+    if parse_public_key_der(bytes).is_ok() {
+        return true;
+    }
+    let Ok((rest, sequence)) = Any::from_der(bytes) else {
+        return false;
+    };
+    if !rest.is_empty() || sequence.tag() != Tag::Sequence {
+        return false;
+    }
+    let Ok((rest, first)) = Any::from_der(sequence.as_bytes()) else {
+        return false;
+    };
+    let Ok((rest, second)) = Any::from_der(rest) else {
+        return false;
+    };
+    if !rest.is_empty() {
+        return false;
+    }
+    // Public PKCS#1 RSA, or opaque EncryptedPrivateKeyInfo. Never decode private bodies.
+    first.tag() == Tag::Integer && second.tag() == Tag::Integer
+        || first.tag() == Tag::Sequence && second.tag() == Tag::OctetString
+}
+

@@ -323,0 +384,39 @@ fn fingerprint(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
+
+fn integer_parameter_fields<'a>(p: &Any<'a>) -> Result<Vec<&'a [u8]>, PublicDerParseError> {
+    if p.tag() != Tag::Sequence {
+        return Err(PublicDerParseError);
+    }
+    let mut input = p.as_bytes();
+    let mut fields = Vec::new();
+    while !input.is_empty() {
+        if fields.len() == 3 {
+            return Err(PublicDerParseError);
+        }
+        let (rest, _) = Any::from_der(input).map_err(|_| PublicDerParseError)?;
+        fields.push(&input[..input.len() - rest.len()]);
+        input = rest;
+    }
+    Ok(fields)
+}
+
+// RFC 9881 and RFC 9909 fixed signature encodings; no signature verification.
+fn pqc_signature_size(oid: &str) -> Option<usize> {
+    let n = oid
+        .strip_prefix("2.16.840.1.101.3.4.3.")?
+        .parse::<u8>()
+        .ok()?;
+    match n {
+        17 => Some(2420),
+        18 => Some(3309),
+        19 => Some(4627),
+        20..=43 => Some(match (n - 20) % 6 {
+            0 => 7856,
+            1 => 17088,
+            2 => 16224,
+            3 => 35664,
+            4 => 29792,
+            _ => 49856,
+        }),
+        _ => None,
+    }
+}

+    let Some(c) = RE.get_or_init(|| Regex::new(r"^v(?:0|[1-9][0-9]*)\.(?:0|[1-9][0-9]*)\.(?:0|[1-9][0-9]*)(?:-([0-9A-Za-z-]+(?:\.[0-9A-Za-z-]+)*))?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z-]+(?:\.[0-9A-Za-z-]+)*)?$").unwrap()).captures(value) else { return false; };

+fn valid_go_metadata(kind: &str, body: &str) -> bool {
+    let fields: Vec<_> = body.split_whitespace().collect();
+    let version = valid_go_module_version;
+    match kind {
+        "tool" => fields.len() == 1 && valid_dependency_name(fields[0], "golang"),
+        "ignore" => fields.len() == 1 && !fields[0].contains(['\0', '"']),
+        "toolchain" => {
+            fields.len() == 1
+                && (fields[0] == "default"
+                    || regex::Regex::new(r"^go[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?$")
+                        .unwrap()
+                        .is_match(fields[0]))
+        }
+        "godebug" => {
+            fields.len() == 1
+                && fields[0].split_once('=').is_some_and(|(k, v)| {
+                    !k.is_empty()
+                        && !v.is_empty()
+                        && k.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == b'_')
+                        && v.bytes()
+                            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"._-".contains(&c))
+                })
+        }
+        "replace" => fields.iter().position(|f| *f == "=>").is_some_and(|i| {
+            (i == 1 || i == 2)
+                && fields.len() > i + 1
+                && fields.len() <= i + 3
+                && valid_dependency_name(fields[0], "golang")
+                && (i == 1 || version(fields[1]))
+                && if fields[i + 1].starts_with('.') || fields[i + 1].starts_with('/') {
+                    fields.len() == i + 2 && !fields[i + 1].contains(['\0', '"'])
+                } else {
+                    fields.len() == i + 3
+                        && valid_dependency_name(fields[i + 1], "golang")
+                        && version(fields[i + 2])
+                }
+        }),
+        "exclude" => {
+            fields.len() == 2 && valid_dependency_name(fields[0], "golang") && version(fields[1])
+        }
+        "retract" => {
+            if let Some(range) = body.strip_prefix('[').and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix(']')) {
+                range
+                    .split_once(',')
+                    .is_some_and(|(lo, hi)| version(lo.trim()) && version(hi.trim()))
+            } else {
+                version(body)
+            }
+        }
+
+        _ => false,
+    }

@@ -347,0 +518,31 @@ fn gemlock(text: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Parsed> {
+// Keep the original platform-qualified record as reported_version. Only a
+// corroborated platform suffix may be removed for the resolved registry version.
+fn gemlock_registry_version<'a>(
+    record: &'a str,
+    platforms: &std::collections::BTreeSet<&str>,
+) -> Option<&'a str> {
+    if resolved_dependency_version("gem", record) {
+        return Some(record);
+    }
+    if record.len() > 256 {
+        return None;
+    }
+    for (offset, byte) in record.bytes().enumerate() {
+        if byte != b'-' {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let suffix = &record[offset + 1..];
+        let declared = suffix != "ruby"
+            && (platforms.contains(suffix)
+                || suffix
+                    .strip_suffix("-gnu")
+                    .filter(|base| base.ends_with("-linux"))
+                    .is_some_and(|base| platforms.contains(base)));
+        let version = &record[..offset];
+        if declared && resolved_dependency_version("gem", version) {
+            return Some(version);
+        }
+    }
+    None
+}
+

@@ -473,0 +779,35 @@ fn pom(text: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Parsed> {
+fn expand_maven(value: &str, properties: &std::collections::BTreeMap<String, String>) -> String {
+    let re = Regex::new(r"\$\{([A-Za-z0-9_.-]+)\}").unwrap();
+    let mut value = value.to_string();
+    for _ in 0..8 {
+        let mut next = String::new();
+        let mut end = 0;
+        for c in re.captures_iter(&value) {
+            let m = c.get(0).unwrap();
+            let replacement = properties
+                .get(&c[1])
+                .map(String::as_str)
+                .unwrap_or(m.as_str());
+            if next
+                .len()
+                .saturating_add(m.start() - end)
+                .saturating_add(replacement.len())
+                > 4096
+            {
+                return value;
+            }
+            next.push_str(&value[end..m.start()]);
+            next.push_str(replacement);
+            end = m.end();
+        }
+        if next.len().saturating_add(value.len() - end) > 4096 {
+            return value;
+        }
+        next.push_str(&value[end..]);
+        if next == value {
+            break;
+        }
+        value = next;
+    }
+    value
+}

@@ -187,0 +172,35 @@ pub(super) fn decode(text: &str, format: Format) -> Result<Value, Failure> {
+
+/// pnpm stores an optional boot graph followed by its project graph. Both
+/// documents share one finite value budget and remain separate inventories.
+pub(super) fn yaml_documents(text: &str) -> Result<Vec<Value>, Failure> {
+    if text.len() > 2 * 1024 * 1024 {
+        return Err(Failure::Limit);
+    }
+    super::yaml_preflight::inspect(text)?;
+    let mut budget = Budget {
+        remaining: 500_000,
+        limited: false,
+    };
+    let mut values = Vec::new();
+    for doc in serde_yaml::Deserializer::from_str(text) {
+        if values.len() == 2 {
+            return Err(Failure::Unsupported);
+        }
+        let seed = Seed {
+            budget: &mut budget,
+            depth: 0,
+        };
+        let value = seed.deserialize(doc).map_err(|_| {
+            if budget.limited {
+                Failure::Limit
+            } else {
+                Failure::Malformed
+            }
+        })?;
+        values.push(value);
+    }
+    if values.is_empty() {
+        return Err(Failure::Malformed);
+    }
+    Ok(values)
+}

+//! Local parent properties only: selected regular files, finite depth, no Maven execution.
+use pqc_scan_core::{RepositoryContext, ScannableFile};
+use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
+pub(super) type Properties = BTreeMap<String, String>;
+pub(super) struct Context {
+    pub properties: Properties,
+    pub profiles: Vec<Properties>,
+    pub limited: bool,
+}
+fn document(text: &str) -> Option<roxmltree::Document<'_>> {
+    if text.contains("<!DOCTYPE") || text.contains("<!ENTITY") {
+        return None;
+    }
+    let d = roxmltree::Document::parse_with_options(
+        text,
+        roxmltree::ParsingOptions {
+            nodes_limit: 50000,
+            ..Default::default()
+        },
+    )
+    .ok()?;
+    if d.descendants().any(|n| n.ancestors().take(66).count() > 65) {
+        return None;
+    }
+    if d.descendants()
+        .filter(|n| n.has_tag_name("properties"))
+        .any(|n| n.children().filter(|n| n.is_element()).take(8193).count() > 8192)
+    {
+        return None;
+    }
+    if d.descendants().filter(|n| n.is_element()).any(|n| {
+        n.tag_name()
+            .namespace()
+            .is_some_and(|ns| ns != "http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0")
+    }) {
+        return None;
+    }
+    Some(d)
+}
+fn field<'a, 'b>(node: roxmltree::Node<'a, 'b>, key: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
+    node.children()
+        .find(|n| n.has_tag_name(key))
+        .and_then(|n| n.text())
+        .map(str::trim)
+}
+pub(super) fn node_properties(node: roxmltree::Node<'_, '_>) -> Properties {
+    let mut out = Properties::new();
+    for n in node
+        .children()
+        .filter(|n| n.has_tag_name("properties"))
+        .flat_map(|n| n.children().filter(|n| n.is_element()))
+    {
+        let key = n.tag_name().name();
+        let value = n.text().unwrap_or("").trim();
+        if out.get(key).is_some_and(|v| v != value) {
+            out.insert(key.into(), format!("${{{key}}}"));
+        } else {
+            out.insert(key.into(), value.into());
+        }
+    }
+    out
+}
+
+pub(super) fn load(file: &ScannableFile, repo: Option<&RepositoryContext>) -> Context {
+    let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut bytes = 0;
+    visit(file, repo, &mut seen, &mut bytes, 0)
+}
+fn visit(
+    file: &ScannableFile,
+    repo: Option<&RepositoryContext>,
+    seen: &mut BTreeSet<std::path::PathBuf>,
+    bytes: &mut usize,
+    depth: usize,
+) -> Context {
+    let mut out = Context {
+        properties: Properties::new(),
+        profiles: vec![],
+        limited: false,
+    };
+    if depth >= 16 || !seen.insert(file.path.clone()) || *bytes > 8 * 1024 * 1024 {
+        out.limited = true;
+        return out;
+    }
+    *bytes += file.bytes.len();
+    if *bytes > 8 * 1024 * 1024 {
+        out.limited = true;
+        return out;
+    }
+    let Some(doc) = file.text.as_deref().and_then(document) else {
+        return out;
+    };
+    let root = doc.root_element();
+    if !root.has_tag_name("project") {
+        return out;
+    }
+    let parent = root.children().find(|n| n.has_tag_name("parent"));
+    if let (Some(p), Some(repo)) = (parent, repo) {
+        let relative = field(p, "relativePath").unwrap_or("../pom.xml");
+        if !relative.is_empty() && !relative.contains(['$', '\0']) {
+            let path = std::path::Path::new(relative);
+            let parent_file = repo
+                .read_relative(&file.path, path)
+                .or_else(|| repo.read_relative(&file.path, &path.join("pom.xml")));
+            if let Some(parent_file) = parent_file {
+                if let Some(pd) = parent_file.text.as_deref().and_then(document) {
+                    let pr = pd.root_element();
+                    let pp = pr.children().find(|n| n.has_tag_name("parent"));
+                    if ["groupId", "artifactId", "version"].iter().all(|key| {
+                        field(p, key).is_some_and(|v| !v.contains('$'))
+                            && field(p, key)
+                                == field(pr, key).or_else(|| {
+                                    (*key != "artifactId")
+                                        .then(|| pp.and_then(|n| field(n, key)))
+                                        .flatten()
+                                })
+                    }) {
+                        out = visit(&parent_file, Some(repo), seen, bytes, depth + 1);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    let own = node_properties(root);
+    for inherited_profile in &mut out.profiles {
+        for key in own.keys() {
+            inherited_profile.remove(key);
+        }
+    }
+    out.properties.extend(own);
+    for key in ["groupId", "artifactId", "version"] {
+        if let Some(value) = field(root, key).or_else(|| parent.and_then(|n| field(n, key))) {
+            out.properties
+                .insert(format!("project.{key}"), value.into());
+            out.properties.insert(format!("pom.{key}"), value.into());
+        }
+        if let Some(value) = parent.and_then(|n| field(n, key)) {
+            for prefix in ["parent.", "project.parent.", "pom.parent."] {
+                out.properties
+                    .insert(format!("{prefix}{key}"), value.into());
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for p in root
+        .children()
+        .filter(|n| n.has_tag_name("profiles"))
+        .flat_map(|n| n.children().filter(|n| n.has_tag_name("profile")))
+    {
+        if out.profiles.len() >= 128 {
+            out.limited = true;
+            break;
+        }
+        let props = node_properties(p);
+        if !props.is_empty() {
+            out.profiles.push(props);
+        }
+    }
+    out
+}

+    if name == "pnpm-lock.yaml" {
+        let values = match decode::yaml_documents(text) {
+            Ok(values) => values,
+            Err(e) => return failure(e),
+        };
+        let mut combined = Parsed {
+            items: Vec::new(),
+            status: DetectorStatus::Complete,
+        };
+        let multiple_documents = values.len() > 1;
+        let mut version = None;
+        for value in values {
+            object(&value)?;
+            let current = value
+                .get("lockfileVersion")
+                .and_then(|v| {
+                    v.as_str()
+                        .map(str::to_string)
+                        .or_else(|| v.as_f64().map(|n| n.to_string()))
+                })
+                .map(|s| {
+                    if s == "9.0" {
+                        "9".into()
+                    } else if s == "6.0" {
+                        "6".into()
+                    } else {
+                        s
+                    }
+                });
+            if multiple_documents && current.is_none() {
+                return failure(Failure::Unsupported);
+            }
+            if version
+                .as_ref()
+                .is_some_and(|v| Some(v) != current.as_ref())
+            {
+                return failure(Failure::Unsupported);
+            }
+            version = current;
+            let parsed = pnpm(&value, text)?;
+            let rank = |status: DetectorStatus| match status {
+                DetectorStatus::Truncated => 5,
+                DetectorStatus::Unsupported => 4,
+                DetectorStatus::Partial => 3,
+                DetectorStatus::NotReported => 2,
+                _ => 1,
+            };
+            if rank(parsed.status) > rank(combined.status) {
+                combined.status = parsed.status;
+            }
+            for mut item in parsed.items {
+                if combined.items.len() >= 10_000 {
+                    combined.status = DetectorStatus::Truncated;
+                    break;
+                }
+                item.ordinal = combined.items.len();
+                combined.items.push(item);
+            }
+        }
+        return Ok(combined);
+    }

+                items: Vec::new(),
+                status: DetectorStatus::NotApplicable,
+            });
+        }

+                                    continue;
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+                            (name.as_str(), spec)
+                        };

+//! Ruby declaration inventory, never DSL execution or installation resolution.
+use super::parse::*;
+use pqc_scan_core::RepositoryContext;
+use tree_sitter::{Node, Parser};
+fn literal<'a>(node: Node<'_>, text: &'a str) -> Option<&'a str> {
+    if node.kind() != "string" {
+        return None;
+    }
+    let raw = text.get(node.byte_range())?;
+    let q = *raw.as_bytes().first()?;
+    if !matches!(q, b'\'' | b'"') || raw.len() < 2 || raw.as_bytes().last() != Some(&q) {
+        return None;
+    }
+    let value = &raw[1..raw.len() - 1];
+    (!value.contains("#{") && !value.contains(['\\', '\n'])).then_some(value)
+}
+fn method<'a>(node: Node<'_>, text: &'a str) -> Option<&'a str> {
+    node.child_by_field_name("method")
+        .and_then(|n| text.get(n.byte_range()))
+}
+fn bound_spec(node: Node<'_>, receiver: &str, text: &str) -> bool {
+    for n in std::iter::successors(Some(node), |n| n.parent()).take(64) {
+        if matches!(n.kind(), "method" | "singleton_method") {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if !matches!(n.kind(), "do_block" | "block") {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let Some(parameters) = n.child_by_field_name("parameters") else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        let raw = text[parameters.byte_range()].trim_matches('|').trim();
+        if !raw
+            .split(|c: char| !c.is_alphanumeric() && c != '_')
+            .any(|part| part == receiver)
+        {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let Some(call) = n.parent() else { return false };
+        return raw == receiver
+            && method(call, text) == Some("new")
+            && call
+                .child_by_field_name("receiver")
+                .and_then(|r| text.get(r.byte_range()))
+                == Some("Gem::Specification");
+    }
+    false
+}
+
+fn declaration_block(n: Node<'_>, text: &str) -> bool {
+    match method(n, text) {
+        Some("group" | "platform" | "platforms" | "source") => {
+            n.child_by_field_name("receiver").is_none()
+        }
+        Some("new") => {
+            n.child_by_field_name("receiver")
+                .and_then(|r| text.get(r.byte_range()))
+                == Some("Gem::Specification")
+        }
+        _ => false,
+    }
+}
+pub(super) fn inspect(
+    file: &ScannableFile,
+    repo: Option<&RepositoryContext>,
+) -> anyhow::Result<Parsed> {
+    let Some(text) = file.text.as_deref() else {
+        return failure(Failure::Unsupported);
+    };
+    if text.len() > 2 * 1024 * 1024 {
+        return failure(Failure::Limit);
+    }
+    let mut parser = Parser::new();
+    parser.set_language(&tree_sitter_ruby::LANGUAGE.into())?;
+    let Some(tree) = parser.parse(text, None) else {
+        return failure(Failure::Limit);
+    };
+    let constraint = Regex::new(
+        r"^(?:(?:~>|>=|<=|!=|==|=|>|<)\s*)?[0-9]+(?:\.[A-Za-z0-9]+)*(?:-[A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$",
+    )?;
+    let mut rebound = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut nodes = vec![tree.root_node()];
+    let mut preflight = 200000usize;
+    while let Some(n) = nodes.pop() {
+        if preflight == 0 {
+            return failure(Failure::Limit);
+        }
+        preflight -= 1;
+        if matches!(
+            n.kind(),
+            "assignment" | "operator_assignment" | "multiple_assignment"
+        ) {
+            if let Some(left) = n.child_by_field_name("left") {
+                let mut bindings = vec![left];
+                while let Some(binding) = bindings.pop() {
+                    if preflight == 0 {
+                        return failure(Failure::Limit);
+                    }
+                    preflight -= 1;
+                    if binding.kind() == "identifier" {
+                        rebound.insert(text[binding.byte_range()].to_string());
+                    } else if !matches!(binding.kind(), "call" | "element_reference") {
+                        let mut c = binding.walk();
+                        bindings.extend(binding.named_children(&mut c));
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        let mut c = n.walk();
+        nodes.extend(n.named_children(&mut c));
+    }
+    let mut b = Build::new(DetectorStatus::Complete);
+    let mut incomplete = tree.root_node().has_error();
+    let mut stack = vec![tree.root_node()];
+    let mut work = 200000usize;
+    while let Some(n) = stack.pop() {
+        if work == 0 {
+            b.status = DetectorStatus::Truncated;
+            break;
+        }
+        work -= 1;
+        if n.kind() == "call" {
+            let name = method(n, text).unwrap_or("");
+            if matches!(
+                name,
+                "eval"
+                    | "instance_eval"
+                    | "class_eval"
+                    | "send"
+                    | "public_send"
+                    | "eval_gemfile"
+                    | "load"
+            ) {
+                incomplete = true;
+            }
+            if name == "gemspec" && n.child_by_field_name("receiver").is_none() {
+                let simple = n.child_by_field_name("arguments").is_none();
+                let candidates = repo
+                    .map(|r| r.sibling_files(&file.path, "gemspec"))
+                    .unwrap_or_default();
+                // Delegated dependencies retain the gemspec's own source path/positions in
+                // its separately selected scan; this directive must not relabel them here.
+                if !simple || candidates.len() != 1 {
+                    incomplete = true;
+                } else if let Some(repo) = repo {
+                    if let Some(child) = repo
+                        .read_relative(&file.path, Path::new(candidates[0].file_name().unwrap()))
+                    {
+                        let child = inspect(&child, None)?;
+                        if child.status != DetectorStatus::Complete {
+                            incomplete = true;
+                        }
+                        if child.status == DetectorStatus::Truncated {
+                            b.status = DetectorStatus::Truncated;
+                        }
+                    } else {
+                        incomplete = true;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            if n.child_by_field_name("receiver").is_none()
+                && !matches!(
+                    name,
+                    "gem" | "gemspec" | "group" | "platforms" | "platform" | "source" | "ruby"
+                )
+            {
+                incomplete = true;
+            }
+            let gem = name == "gem" && n.child_by_field_name("receiver").is_none();
+            let spec = matches!(
+                name,
+                "add_dependency" | "add_runtime_dependency" | "add_development_dependency"
+            ) && n
+                .child_by_field_name("receiver")
+                .and_then(|r| text.get(r.byte_range()))
+                .is_some_and(|r| !rebound.contains(r) && bound_spec(n, r, text));
+            if !spec
+                && matches!(
+                    name,
+                    "add_dependency"
+                        | "add_runtime_dependency"
+                        | "add_development_dependency"
+                        | "dependencies"
+                        | "runtime_dependencies"
+                        | "development_dependencies"
+                )
+            {
+                incomplete = true;
+            }
+            if gem || spec {
+                let Some(args) = n.child_by_field_name("arguments") else {
+                    incomplete = true;
+                    continue;
+                };
+                let mut c = args.walk();
+                let args: Vec<_> = args.named_children(&mut c).collect();
+                let Some(dep) = args.first().and_then(|n| literal(*n, text)) else {
+                    incomplete = true;
+                    continue;
+                };
+                let mut conditional =
+                    std::iter::successors(Some(n), |n| n.parent())
+                        .take(64)
+                        .any(|p| {
+                            matches!(
+                                p.kind(),
+                                "if" | "unless" | "if_modifier" | "unless_modifier" | "case"
+                            ) || p.kind() == "call"
+                                && matches!(
+                                    method(p, text),
+                                    Some("group" | "platforms" | "platform")
+                                )
+                        });
+                let deferred = std::iter::successors(Some(n), |n| n.parent())
+                    .take(64)
+                    .any(|p| {
+                        matches!(
+                            p.kind(),
+                            "method"
+                                | "singleton_method"
+                                | "lambda"
+                                | "while"
+                                | "until"
+                                | "while_modifier"
+                                | "until_modifier"
+                                | "for"
+                        ) || p.kind() == "call"
+                            && p.child_by_field_name("block").is_some()
+                            && !declaration_block(p, text)
+                    });
+                conditional |= deferred;
+                incomplete |= deferred;
+                let mut origin = if conditional {
+                    Origin::ManifestOptional
+                } else {
+                    Origin::Manifest
+                };
+                let mut versions = vec![];
+                let mut dynamic = false;
+                for arg in args.iter().skip(1) {
+                    if let Some(value) = literal(*arg, text) {
+                        versions.push(value.to_string());
+                    } else if arg.kind() == "pair" {
+                        if let Some(key) = arg
+                            .child_by_field_name("key")
+                            .and_then(|k| text.get(k.byte_range()))
+                        {
+                            match key.trim_start_matches(':') {
+                                "platform" | "platforms" => {
+                                    conditional = true;
+                                    if matches!(origin, Origin::Manifest) {
+                                        origin = Origin::ManifestOptional;
+                                    }
+                                }
+                                "git" => origin = Origin::Git,
+                                "path" => origin = Origin::Path,
+                                _ => {}
+                            }
+                        }
+                    } else {
+                        dynamic = true;
+                    }
+                }
+                dynamic |= versions.iter().any(|v| !constraint.is_match(v.trim()));
+                incomplete |= dynamic;
+                let constraint = versions.join(", ");
+                b.add(
+                    "gem",
+                    dep,
+                    (!dynamic && !constraint.is_empty()).then_some(constraint.as_str()),
+                    (!dynamic && !constraint.is_empty()).then_some(constraint.as_str()),
+                    None,
+                    if dynamic { Origin::Unknown } else { origin },
+                    n.start_position().row + 1,
+                    false,
+                );
+                if let Some(item) = b.items.last_mut() {
+                    item.context = if deferred {
+                        Some("deferred_declaration_not_evaluated")
+                    } else if dynamic {
+                        Some("dynamic_version_not_evaluated")
+                    } else if conditional {
+                        Some("conditional_declaration_not_effective")
+                    } else {
+                        None
+                    };
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        let mut cursor = n.walk();
+        for child in n.named_children(&mut cursor) {
+            stack.push(child);
+        }
+    }
+    b.items.sort_by_key(|i| i.line);
+    for (ordinal, item) in b.items.iter_mut().enumerate() {
+        item.ordinal = ordinal;
+    }
+    if incomplete && b.status != DetectorStatus::Truncated {
+        b.status = DetectorStatus::Partial;
+    }
+    b.finish()
+}
+use std::path::Path;

+//! Reject actual YAML node properties before deserialization, without treating
+//! punctuation inside plain/quoted/block scalars as anchors, aliases or tags.
+//! Grammar reference: https://yaml.org/spec/1.2.2/#733-plain-style
+use super::decode::Failure;
+pub(super) fn inspect(text: &str) -> Result<(), Failure> {
+    let mut quote = None;
+    let mut escaped = false;
+    let mut flow = 0usize;
+    let mut block_indent = None;
+    for line in text.lines() {
+        let indent = line.len() - line.trim_start().len();
+        if indent > 256 {
+            return Err(Failure::Limit);
+        }
+        if let Some(parent) = block_indent {
+            if line.trim().is_empty() || indent > parent {
+                continue;
+            }
+            block_indent = None;
+        }
+        // A document marker is not a plain scalar. Properties on the same
+        // line belong to the new root node and must receive the same guard.
+        let content = if quote.is_none()
+            && (line.starts_with("---") || line.starts_with("..."))
+            && line.as_bytes().get(3).is_none_or(u8::is_ascii_whitespace)
+        {
+            &line[3..]
+        } else {
+            line
+        };
+        let mut plain = false;
+        let mut quoted = false;
+        let mut previous = None;
+        let mut chars = content.chars().peekable();
+        while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
+            if let Some(q) = quote {
+                if escaped {
+                    escaped = false;
+                } else if c == '\\' && q == '"' {
+                    escaped = true;
+                } else if c == q {
+                    // YAML single quotes escape themselves by doubling.
+                    if q == '\'' && chars.peek() == Some(&'\'') {
+                        chars.next();
+                    } else {
+                        quote = None;
+                        quoted = true;
+                    }
+                }
+                continue;
+            }
+            if c == '#' && previous.is_none_or(char::is_whitespace) {
+                break;
+            }
+            if c.is_whitespace() {
+                previous = Some(c);
+                continue;
+            }
+            if c == ':'
+                && (quoted
+                    || chars
+                        .peek()
+                        .is_none_or(|n| n.is_whitespace() || "[]{},".contains(*n)))
+            {
+                plain = false;
+                quoted = false;
+                previous = Some(c);
+                continue;
+            }
+            if matches!(c, '[' | '{') && (!plain || flow > 0) {
+                flow += 1;
+                if flow > 64 {
+                    return Err(Failure::Limit);
+                }
+                plain = false;
+                quoted = false;
+                previous = Some(c);
+                continue;
+            }
+            if flow > 0 && matches!(c, ']' | '}' | ',') {
+                if c != ',' {
+                    flow = flow.saturating_sub(1);
+                }
+                plain = false;
+                quoted = false;
+                previous = Some(c);
+                continue;
+            }
+            if !plain {
+                if matches!(c, '&' | '*' | '!') {
+                    return Err(Failure::Unsupported);
+                }
+                if matches!(c, '\'' | '"') {
+                    quote = Some(c);
+                    quoted = false;
+                    previous = Some(c);
+                    continue;
+                }
+                if matches!(c, '|' | '>') && flow == 0 {
+                    block_indent = Some(indent);
+                    break;
+                }
+                if matches!(c, '-' | '?') && chars.peek().is_none_or(|n| n.is_whitespace()) {
+                    previous = Some(c);
+                    continue;
+                }
+                plain = true;
+                quoted = false;
+            }
+            previous = Some(c);
+        }
+    }
+    Ok(())
+}

diff --git a/crates/pqc_scan_detectors/src/regex_detector/go_imports.rs b/crates/pqc_scan_detectors/src/regex_detector/go_imports.rs

+//! One bounded parse per Go file, shared by the built-in import indicators.
+use pqc_scan_core::DetectorStatus;
+
+pub(super) struct Imports {
+    pub spans: Vec<(usize, usize)>,
+    pub status: DetectorStatus,
+}
+pub(super) fn collect(text: &str, max_nodes: usize) -> Imports {
+    let mut result = Imports {
+        spans: Vec::new(),
+        status: DetectorStatus::Complete,
+    };
+    let mut parser = tree_sitter::Parser::new();
+    if parser
+        .set_language(&tree_sitter_go::LANGUAGE.into())
+        .is_err()
+    {
+        result.status = DetectorStatus::Partial;
+        return result;
+    }
+    let Some(tree) = parser.parse(text, None) else {
+        result.status = DetectorStatus::Partial;
+        return result;
+    };
+    let mut remaining = max_nodes;
+    let mut cursor = tree.root_node().walk();
+    'declarations: for declaration in tree.root_node().named_children(&mut cursor) {
+        let Some(next) = remaining.checked_sub(1) else {
+            result.status = DetectorStatus::Truncated;
+            break;
+        };
+        remaining = next;
+        if declaration.kind() != "import_declaration" {
+            continue;
+        }
+        if declaration.has_error() {
+            result.status = DetectorStatus::Partial;
+        }
+        let mut stack = vec![declaration];
+        while let Some(node) = stack.pop() {
+            let Some(next) = remaining.checked_sub(1) else {
+                result.status = DetectorStatus::Truncated;
+                break 'declarations;
+            };
+            remaining = next;
+            if node.kind() == "import_spec" && !node.has_error() {
+                if let Some(path) = node.child_by_field_name("path") {
+                    let start = path.start_byte() + 1;
+                    let end = path.end_byte().saturating_sub(1);
+                    if start <= end && text.get(start..end) == Some("crypto/rsa") {
+                        result.spans.push((start, end));
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            let mut c = node.walk();
+            for child in node.named_children(&mut c) {
+                stack.push(child);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    result.spans.sort_unstable();
+    result
+}

+//! Content corroborates Dockerfile variant names before overriding an extension.
+use std::path::Path;
+
+pub(crate) fn is_dockerfile(path: &Path, text: &str) -> bool {
+    let Some(name) = path.file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()) else {
+        return false;
+    };
+    let name = name.to_ascii_lowercase();
+    if name != "dockerfile"
+        && name
+            .strip_prefix("dockerfile.")
+            .is_none_or(|s| s.is_empty())
+    {
+        return false;
+    }
+    let Some(line) = text
+        .trim_start_matches('\u{feff}')
+        .lines()
+        .map(str::trim)
+        .find(|line| !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with('#'))
+    else {
+        return false;
+    };
+    let Some((instruction, argument)) = line.split_once(char::is_whitespace) else {
+        return false;
+    };
+    let argument = argument.trim();
+    if instruction.eq_ignore_ascii_case("ARG") {
+        let name = argument.split('=').next().unwrap_or("");
+        return !name.is_empty()
+            && name
+                .bytes()
+                .enumerate()
+                .all(|(i, b)| b == b'_' || b.is_ascii_alphabetic() || i > 0 && b.is_ascii_digit());
+    }
+    if !instruction.eq_ignore_ascii_case("FROM") {
+        return false;
+    }
+    let mut words = argument.split_whitespace();
+    let Some(mut image) = words.next() else {
+        return false;
+    };
+    if image.starts_with("--platform=") {
+        let Some(next) = words.next() else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        image = next;
+    }
+    if image.is_empty() || image.starts_with(['=', '-']) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    match words.next() {
+        None => true,
+        Some(word) if word.eq_ignore_ascii_case("AS") => {
+            words.next().is_some() && words.next().is_none()
+        }
+        _ => false,
+    }
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {
+    #[test]
+    fn python_from_import_is_source_even_with_a_dockerfile_name() {
+        assert!(!super::is_dockerfile(
+            std::path::Path::new("Dockerfile.py"),
+            "from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes\n"
+        ));
+        assert!(!super::is_dockerfile(
+            std::path::Path::new("Dockerfile.py"),
+            "from cryptography import (hashes, rsa)\n"
+        ));
+        for text in [
+            "FROM alpine",
+            "FROM --platform=$BUILDPLATFORM alpine AS build",
+            "ARG BASE=alpine\nFROM ${BASE}",
+        ] {
+            assert!(super::is_dockerfile(
+                std::path::Path::new("Dockerfile.py"),
+                text
+            ));
+        }
+    }
+}

+//! Parse-only view of unrendered template source. Never chooses a branch/value.
+pub(crate) struct View {
+    pub bytes: Vec<u8>,
+    pub found: bool,
+    pub limited: bool,
+    pub conditional_ranges: Vec<(usize, usize)>,
+}
+pub(crate) fn view(text: &str, tree: &tree_sitter::Tree) -> View {
+    view_with_comments(text, false, Some(tree))
+}
+pub(crate) fn requirements_view(text: &str) -> View {
+    view_with_comments(text, true, None)
+}
+fn view_with_comments(text: &str, hash_comments: bool, tree: Option<&tree_sitter::Tree>) -> View {
+    if !text.contains("{{") && !text.contains("{%") {
+        return View {
+            bytes: vec![],
+            found: false,
+            limited: false,
+            conditional_ranges: vec![],
+        };
+    }
+    let mut strong = false;
+    let line_offsets: Vec<_> = text.match_indices('\n').map(|(i, _)| i).collect();
+    let mut blocks: Vec<(&str, usize)> = vec![];
+    let mut outer_start = 0;
+    let s = text.as_bytes();
+    let mut out = View {
+        bytes: s.to_vec(),
+        found: false,
+        limited: false,
+        conditional_ranges: vec![],
+    };
+    let mut i = 0;
+    let mut count = 0;
+    'scan: while i < s.len() {
+        if s[i] == b'/' {
+            if let Some(mut node) =
+                tree.and_then(|t| t.root_node().descendant_for_byte_range(i, i + 1))
+            {
+                for _ in 0..64 {
+                    if node.kind() == "regex" {
+                        i = node.end_byte();
+                        continue 'scan;
+                    }
+                    if let Some(parent) = node.parent() {
+                        node = parent;
+                    } else {
+                        break;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if hash_comments && s[i] == b'#' {
+            while i < s.len() && s[i] != b'\n' {
+                i += 1;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+        if matches!(s[i], b'\'' | b'"' | b'`') {
+            let q = s[i];
+            i += 1;
+            while i < s.len() {
+                let c = s[i];
+                i += 1;
+                if c == b'\\' {
+                    i = (i + 1).min(s.len());
+                } else if c == q {
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+        if s[i..].starts_with(b"//") {
+            while i < s.len() && s[i] != b'\n' {
+                i += 1;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+        if s[i..].starts_with(b"/*") {
+            i += 2;
+            while i < s.len() && !s[i..].starts_with(b"*/") {
+                i += 1;
+            }
+            i = (i + 2).min(s.len());
+            continue;
+        }
+        let directive = s[i..].starts_with(b"{%");
+        let expression = s[i..].starts_with(b"{{");
+        if !directive && !expression {
+            i += 1;
+            continue;
+        }
+        count += 1;
+        if count > 10000 {
+            out.limited = strong;
+            break;
+        }
+        let closing = if directive { b"%}" } else { b"}}" };
+        let Some(end) = s[i + 2..]
+            .windows(2)
+            .position(|w| w == closing)
+            .map(|n| i + 2 + n + 2)
+        else {
+            break;
+        };
+        let body = text.get(i + 2..end - 2).unwrap_or("").trim();
+        let recognized = directive
+            || body.starts_with('$')
+            || (!body.is_empty()
+                && body
+                    .chars()
+                    .all(|c| c.is_alphanumeric() || "_ .|".contains(c)));
+        if !recognized {
+            i = end;
+            continue;
+        }
+        if hash_comments && directive {
+            let tag = body
+                .trim_matches(['-', '+'])
+                .split_whitespace()
+                .next()
+                .unwrap_or("");
+            let starts = matches!(
+                tag,
+                "if" | "for" | "with" | "block" | "macro" | "filter" | "call"
+            );
+            if starts {
+                if blocks.len() >= 64 {
+                    out.limited = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                if blocks.is_empty() {
+                    outer_start = 1 + line_offsets.partition_point(|n| *n < i);
+                }
+                blocks.push((tag, outer_start));
+            } else if matches!(
+                tag,
+                "endif" | "endfor" | "endwith" | "endblock" | "endmacro" | "endfilter" | "endcall"
+            ) {
+                blocks.pop();
+                if blocks.is_empty() {
+                    out.conditional_ranges
+                        .push((outer_start, 1 + line_offsets.partition_point(|n| *n < end)));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        strong |= directive || body.starts_with('$') && body.contains(":=");
+        out.found = true;
+        for byte in &mut out.bytes[i..end] {
+            if !matches!(*byte, b'\n' | b'\r') {
+                *byte = b' ';
+            }
+        }
+        if expression && !body.contains(":=") {
+            out.bytes[i] = b'_';
+        }
+        i = end;
+    }
+    if !blocks.is_empty() {
+        out.conditional_ranges
+            .push((outer_start, line_offsets.len() + 1));
+    }
+    out.found &= strong;
+    out
+}

+//! A narrow guard for the sloppy-yield compatibility, not a JS compiler.
+use tree_sitter::{Node, Tree};
+fn directive(node: Node<'_>, text: &str) -> bool {
+    let mut cursor = node.walk();
+    for n in node.named_children(&mut cursor) {
+        if n.kind() == "comment" {
+            continue;
+        }
+        if n.kind() != "expression_statement" {
+            break;
+        }
+        let Some(value) = n.named_child(0).filter(|n| n.kind() == "string") else {
+            break;
+        };
+        if matches!(&text[value.byte_range()], "'use strict'" | "\"use strict\"") {
+            return true;
+        }
+    }
+    false
+}
+pub(super) fn strict_yield_binding(
+    text: &str,
+    tree: &Tree,
+    module: bool,
+    max: usize,
+) -> (bool, bool) {
+    let root = tree.root_node();
+    let mut c = root.walk();
+    let strict = module
+        || directive(root, text)
+        || root
+            .named_children(&mut c)
+            .any(|n| matches!(n.kind(), "import_statement" | "export_statement"))
+        || text
+            .lines()
+            .take(10)
+            .any(|l| l.trim_start().starts_with("// Flags:") && l.contains("--use-strict"));
+    let mut stack = vec![(root, strict)];
+    let mut examined = 0;
+    while let Some((n, inherited)) = stack.pop() {
+        if examined >= max {
+            return (false, true);
+        }
+        examined += 1;
+        let strict = inherited
+            || matches!(n.kind(), "class" | "class_declaration")
+            || matches!(
+                n.kind(),
+                "function_declaration"
+                    | "function_expression"
+                    | "generator_function_declaration"
+                    | "generator_function"
+                    | "arrow_function"
+                    | "method_definition"
+            ) && n
+                .child_by_field_name("body")
+                .is_some_and(|b| b.kind() == "statement_block" && directive(b, text));
+        if strict
+            && n.kind() == "identifier"
+            && &text[n.byte_range()] == "yield"
+            && n.parent().is_some_and(|p| {
+                matches!(
+                    p.kind(),
+                    "variable_declarator"
+                        | "formal_parameters"
+                        | "required_parameter"
+                        | "optional_parameter"
+                        | "function_declaration"
+                        | "function_expression"
+                )
+            })
+        {
+            return (true, false);
+        }
+        let mut c = n.walk();
+        stack.extend(n.named_children(&mut c).map(|n| (n, strict)));
+    }
+    (false, false)
+}

+//! Parse-only compatibility for module modifiers absent from the pinned grammars.
+//! The original source is always used for matching, evidence and coordinates.
+use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
+use tree_sitter::{Parser, Tree};
+
+fn token(text: &str, mut i: usize) -> Option<(&str, usize, usize)> {
+    let b = text.as_bytes();
+    loop {
+        while b.get(i).is_some_and(u8::is_ascii_whitespace) {
+            i += 1;
+        }
+        if b.get(i..i + 2) == Some(b"/*") {
+            i += 2;
+            i += text.get(i..)?.find("*/")? + 2;
+        } else if b.get(i..i + 2) == Some(b"//") {
+            i += text.get(i..)?.find('\n')? + 1;
+        } else {
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+    let start = i;
+    let c = *b.get(i)?;
+    if c.is_ascii_alphabetic() || c == b'_' || c == b'$' {
+        i += 1;
+        while b
+            .get(i)
+            .is_some_and(|v| v.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || *v == b'_' || *v == b'$')
+        {
+            i += 1;
+        }
+    } else {
+        i += 1;
+    }
+    Some((text.get(start..i)?, start, i))
+}
+pub(super) fn compatible_tree(
+    parser: &mut Parser,
+    text: &str,
+    tree: Tree,
+    language: &str,
+    work_budget: usize,
+    module: bool,
+) -> Result<(Tree, bool)> {
+    if !tree.root_node().has_error() {
+        return Ok((tree, false));
+    }
+    let flow = language == "javascript"
+        && text
+            .lines()
+            .take(5)
+            .any(|l| l.trim_start().starts_with("//") && l.contains("@flow"));
+    if flow {
+        parser
+            .set_language(&tree_sitter_typescript::LANGUAGE_TSX.into())
+            .map_err(|_| anyhow!("failed to initialize Flow-compatible parser"))?;
+    }
+    let mut changes = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+    // Only actual import/export statements (or top-level error spans) qualify.
+    // Strings, comments, templates and nested arbitrary error fragments do not.
+    let root = tree.root_node();
+    let mut cursor = root.walk();
+    for node in root.named_children(&mut cursor) {
+        if !matches!(
+            node.kind(),
+            "export_statement" | "import_statement" | "ERROR"
+        ) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let start = node.start_byte();
+        let fragment = &text[start..node.end_byte()];
+        let Some((first, _, end)) = token(fragment, 0) else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        let Some((modifier, a, end)) = token(fragment, end) else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        let Some((star, _, _)) = token(fragment, end) else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        let supported = (language == "typescript" && first == "export" && modifier == "type")
+            || (first == "import" && modifier == "defer");
+        if supported && star == "*" {
+            changes.insert((start + a, start + end));
+        }
+    }
+    drop(cursor);
+    let (mut bytes, limited) =
+        super::syntax_compat::normalize(text, &tree, language, work_budget, module);
+    if limited {
+        return Ok((tree, true));
+    }
+    if changes.is_empty() && bytes == text.as_bytes() && !flow {
+        return Ok((tree, false));
+    }
+    for (a, b) in changes {
+        bytes[a..b].fill(b' ');
+    }
+    // Replacing ASCII modifiers with spaces preserves every byte, line and column.
+    parser
+        .parse(&bytes, None)
+        .map(|tree| (tree, false))
+        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("source parser produced no tree"))
+}
