+//! Bounded static import binding; no imports, target code or package managers run.
+use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
+use tree_sitter::{Node, Tree};
+
+pub(super) struct ResolvedCall {
+    pub start: usize,
+    pub end: usize,
+    pub canonical: String,
+}
+struct Work(usize);
+impl Work {
+    fn spend(&mut self) -> Option<()> {
+        self.0 = self.0.checked_sub(1)?;
+        Some(())
+    }
+}
+
+pub(super) fn aliases(
+    text: &str,
+    tree: &Tree,
+    language: &str,
+    max_nodes: usize,
+    max_calls: usize,
+) -> (Vec<ResolvedCall>, bool) {
+    if language != "python" {
+        return (Vec::new(), false);
+    }
+    let mut work = Work(max_nodes.saturating_mul(8));
+    match resolve(text, tree, &mut work, max_calls) {
+        Some(calls) => (calls, false),
+        None => (Vec::new(), true),
+    }
+}
+fn resolve(
+    text: &str,
+    tree: &Tree,
+    work: &mut Work,
+    max_calls: usize,
+) -> Option<Vec<ResolvedCall>> {
+    let mut imports = BTreeMap::new();
+    let mut rebound = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut ambiguous = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut wildcard_scopes = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut calls = Vec::new();
+    let mut stack = vec![tree.root_node()];
+    while let Some(node) = stack.pop() {
+        work.spend()?;
+        match node.kind() {
+            "import_from_statement" if !node.has_error() => {
+                let module = node
+                    .child_by_field_name("module_name")
+                    .map(|n| text[n.byte_range()].to_string())
+                    .unwrap_or_default();
+                let scope = scopes(node, work, false)?[0];
+                let mut c = node.walk();
+                if node
+                    .named_children(&mut c)
+                    .any(|n| n.kind() == "wildcard_import")
+                {
+                    wildcard_scopes.insert(scope);
+                }
+                let mut c = node.walk();
+                for item in node.children_by_field_name("name", &mut c) {
+                    if let Some((name, alias)) = import_name(item, text) {
+                        let local = alias.unwrap_or_else(|| name.clone());
+                        remember(
+                            &mut imports,
+                            &mut rebound,
+                            scope,
+                            local,
+                            supported_module(&module).then(|| format!("{module}.{name}")),
+                        );
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            "import_statement" if !node.has_error() => {
+                let scope = scopes(node, work, false)?[0];
+                let mut c = node.walk();
+                for item in node.children_by_field_name("name", &mut c) {
+                    if let Some((name, alias)) = import_name(item, text) {
+                        let supported = supported_module(&name);
+                        let (local, canonical) = if let Some(alias) = alias {
+                            (alias, name)
+                        } else {
+                            let root = name.split('.').next()?.to_string();
+                            (root.clone(), root)
+                        };
+                        remember(
+                            &mut imports,
+                            &mut rebound,
+                            scope,
+                            local,
+                            supported.then_some(canonical),
+                        );
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            "assignment"
+            | "augmented_assignment"
+            | "named_expression"
+            | "for_statement"
+            | "for_in_clause"
+            | "as_pattern" => {
+                if let Some(left) = node
+                    .child_by_field_name("left")
+                    .or_else(|| node.child_by_field_name("name"))
+                    .or_else(|| node.child_by_field_name("alias"))
+                {
+                    bindings(
+                        left,
+                        text,
+                        scopes(node, work, false)?[0],
+                        &mut rebound,
+                        work,
+                    )?;
+                }
+            }
+            "function_definition" | "class_definition" => {
+                if let Some(name) = node.child_by_field_name("name") {
+                    rebound.insert((
+                        scopes(node.parent()?, work, false)?[0],
+                        text[name.byte_range()].to_string(),
+                    ));
+                }
+            }
+            "parameters" | "lambda_parameters" => bindings(
+                node,
+                text,
+                scopes(node, work, false)?[0],
+                &mut rebound,
+                work,
+            )?,
+            "global_statement" | "nonlocal_statement" => {
+                let mut c = node.walk();
+                for child in node.named_children(&mut c) {
+                    if child.kind() == "identifier" {
+                        ambiguous.insert(text[child.byte_range()].to_string());
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            "call" => {
+                if calls.len() >= max_calls {
+                    return None;
+                }
+                if let Some(function) = node.child_by_field_name("function") {
+                    calls.push(function);
+                }
+            }
+            _ => {}
+        }
+        let mut c = node.walk();
+        for child in node.named_children(&mut c) {
+            stack.push(child);
+        }
+    }
+    let mut out = Vec::new();
+    for function in calls {
+        let raw = &text[function.byte_range()];
+        // Supported qualified call names are short; do not repeatedly split
+        // arbitrarily large computed receivers or copy them into evidence.
+        if raw.len() > 512 {
+            continue;
+        }
+        work.spend()?;
+        let parts = raw.split('.').map(str::trim).collect::<Vec<_>>();
+        if !parts.iter().all(|p| identifier(p)) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let first = parts[0];
+        if ambiguous.contains(first) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let mut resolved = None;
+        for scope in scopes(function, work, true)? {
+            if wildcard_scopes.contains(&scope) || rebound.contains(&(scope, first.to_string())) {
+                break;
+            }
+            if let Some(binding) = imports.get(&(scope, first.to_string())) {
+                resolved = Some(binding);
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        let Some(binding) = resolved else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        let canonical = if parts.len() == 1 {
+            binding.clone()
+        } else {
+            format!("{binding}.{}", parts[1..].join("."))
+        };
+        if canonical.len() > 256 {
+            continue;
+        }
+        out.push(ResolvedCall {
+            start: function.start_byte(),
+            end: function.end_byte(),
+            canonical,
+        });
+    }
+    Some(out)
+}
+fn import_name(node: Node<'_>, text: &str) -> Option<(String, Option<String>)> {
+    let (name, alias) = if node.kind() == "aliased_import" {
+        (
+            node.child_by_field_name("name")?,
+            node.child_by_field_name("alias"),
+        )
+    } else {
+        (node, None)
+    };
+    let name = text[name.byte_range()]
+        .split('.')
+        .map(str::trim)
+        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
+        .join(".");
+    if !name.split('.').all(identifier) {
+        return None;
+    }
+    Some((name, alias.map(|a| text[a.byte_range()].to_string())))
+}
+fn supported_module(module: &str) -> bool {
+    module.len() <= 128
+        && (module == "cryptography"
+            || module == "Crypto"
+            || module == "Crypto.PublicKey"
+            || module.starts_with("Crypto.PublicKey.")
+            || module == "cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric"
+            || module.starts_with("cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric."))
+}
+fn identifier(s: &str) -> bool {
+    let mut chars = s.chars();
+    chars.next().is_some_and(|c| c == '_' || c.is_alphabetic())
+        && chars.all(|c| c == '_' || c.is_alphanumeric())
+}
+fn scopes(mut node: Node<'_>, work: &mut Work, evaluation: bool) -> Option<Vec<usize>> {
+    let origin = (node.start_byte(), node.end_byte());
+    let mut out = Vec::new();
+    let mut in_function = false;
+    loop {
+        work.spend()?;
+        match node.kind() {
+            "function_definition" | "lambda" => {
+                let in_body = node.child_by_field_name("body").is_some_and(|body| {
+                    body.start_byte() <= origin.0 && body.end_byte() >= origin.1
+                });
+                if !evaluation || in_body {
+                    out.push(node.start_byte() + 1);
+                    in_function = true;
+                }
+            }
+            "list_comprehension"
+            | "set_comprehension"
+            | "dictionary_comprehension"
+            | "generator_expression" => out.push(node.start_byte() + 1),
+            "class_definition" if !in_function => {
+                let in_body = node.child_by_field_name("body").is_some_and(|body| {
+                    body.start_byte() <= origin.0 && body.end_byte() >= origin.1
+                });
+                if !evaluation || in_body {
+                    out.push(node.start_byte() + 1);
+                }
+            }
+            _ => {}
+        }
+        if let Some(parent) = node.parent() {
+            node = parent;
+        } else {
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+    out.push(0);
+    Some(out)
+}
+fn bindings(
+    node: Node<'_>,
+    text: &str,
+    scope: usize,
+    out: &mut BTreeSet<(usize, String)>,
+    work: &mut Work,
+) -> Option<()> {
+    let mut pending = vec![node];
+    while let Some(node) = pending.pop() {
+        work.spend()?;
+        if node.kind() == "identifier" {
+            out.insert((scope, text[node.byte_range()].to_string()));
+        }
+        let selected = match node.kind() {
+            "default_parameter" | "typed_default_parameter" => node.child_by_field_name("name"),
+            "attribute" => node.child_by_field_name("object"),
+            "subscript" => node.child_by_field_name("value"),
+            _ => None,
+        };
+        if let Some(name) = selected {
+            pending.push(name);
+            continue;
+        }
+        let annotation = node.child_by_field_name("type");
+        let mut c = node.walk();
+        for child in node.named_children(&mut c) {
+            if annotation.is_none_or(|n| n.id() != child.id()) {
+                pending.push(child);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    Some(())
+}
+
+fn remember(
+    imports: &mut BTreeMap<(usize, String), String>,
+    rebound: &mut BTreeSet<(usize, String)>,
+    scope: usize,
+    name: String,
+    canonical: Option<String>,
+) {
+    let key = (scope, name);
+    if let Some(canonical) = canonical {
+        if imports
+            .insert(key.clone(), canonical.clone())
+            .is_some_and(|previous| previous != canonical)
+        {
+            rebound.insert(key);
+        }
+    } else {
+        rebound.insert(key);
+    }
+}

index 0000000..56f75d9

+//! Parse-only, byte-preserving compatibility for specific grammar limitations.
+//! Matching and evidence always read the original source. This is not evaluation.
+mod javascript;
+mod javascript_lexer;
+mod jsx_attributes;
+mod typescript_types;
+use tree_sitter::Tree;
+macro_rules! re {
+    ($pattern:literal) => {{
+        static RE: std::sync::OnceLock<regex::Regex> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
+        RE.get_or_init(|| regex::Regex::new($pattern).expect("static syntax pattern"))
+    }};
+}
+
+pub(super) fn normalize(
+    text: &str,
+    tree: &Tree,
+    language: &str,
+    work_budget: usize,
+    module: bool,
+) -> (Vec<u8>, bool) {
+    let mut remaining = work_budget;
+    macro_rules! spend {
+        ($n:expr) => {{
+            let n = $n;
+            if n > remaining {
+                return (text.as_bytes().to_vec(), true);
+            }
+            remaining -= n;
+        }};
+    }
+    spend!(text.len().saturating_mul(32));
+    let mut bytes = text.as_bytes().to_vec();
+    let mut protected = vec![false; bytes.len()];
+    let mut jsx = Vec::new();
+    let mut stack = vec![tree.root_node()];
+    while let Some(node) = stack.pop() {
+        spend!(1usize);
+        let kind = node.kind();
+        if kind.contains("string")
+            || kind == "comment"
+            || kind == "char_literal"
+            || kind.contains("heredoc")
+        {
+            spend!(node.end_byte() - node.start_byte());
+            protected[node.byte_range()].fill(true);
+        }
+        if kind.starts_with("jsx_") {
+            jsx.push((node.start_byte(), node.end_byte(), kind.to_string()));
+        }
+        if kind == "ERROR" && node.parent().is_some_and(|p| p.kind() == "jsx_element") {
+            jsx.push((node.start_byte(), node.end_byte(), "jsx_text".into()));
+        }
+        let mut cursor = node.walk();
+        for child in node.named_children(&mut cursor) {
+            stack.push(child);
+        }
+    }
+    if language == "javascript"
+        && !javascript::normalize(text, tree, &mut bytes, &mut remaining, module)
+    {
+        return (text.as_bytes().to_vec(), true);
+    }
+    if language == "go" {
+        // Error recovery can pair unrelated quotes around new(value).
+        // Lexically valid string/comment boundaries do not depend on that tree.
+        let protected = go_lexical_protection(text.as_bytes());
+        let re = re!(r"\bnew\s*\(");
+        for m in re.find_iter(text) {
+            if !protected[m.start()] {
+                bytes[m.start()..m.start() + 3].copy_from_slice(b"n_w");
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if matches!(language, "javascript" | "typescript") {
+        if language == "typescript" {
+            // V8 intrinsics are prefix calls. A preceding value would instead be %.
+            let re = re!(r"%[ \t]*[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*\s*\(");
+            for m in re.find_iter(text) {
+                if protected[m.start()] {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                let before = text[..m.start()].trim_end();
+                let previous = before.chars().next_back();
+                let prefix = previous.is_none_or(|c| "=([{,:;!?&|+-*/%^~<>".contains(c))
+                    || before.ends_with(['\n', '\r'])
+                    || ["return", "throw", "yield", "else"]
+                        .iter()
+                        .any(|w| before.ends_with(w));
+                // In V8, a standalone intrinsic after an if/loop condition is valid.
+                let line = text[..m.start()].rsplit('\n').next().unwrap_or("").trim();
+                if prefix || line.is_empty() || line.starts_with("if (") && line.ends_with(')') {
+                    bytes[m.start()] = b' ';
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        let re = re!(r#"\bimport\s+source\s+([A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*)\s+from\s*['"]"#);
+        for c in re.captures_iter(text) {
+            let m = c.get(0).unwrap();
+            if !protected[m.start()] {
+                let start = m.start() + text[m.range()].find("source").unwrap();
+                bytes[start..start + 6].fill(b' ');
+            }
+        }
+        let re = re!(r"\bimport\.source\s*\(");
+        for m in re.find_iter(text) {
+            if !protected[m.start()] {
+                bytes[m.start() + 6..m.start() + 13].fill(b' ');
+            }
+        }
+        // Preserve quoted text and its positions while avoiding a JSX entity lexer bug.
+        for (start, end, kind) in &jsx {
+            spend!(end - start);
+            if kind == "jsx_text" {
+                for byte in &mut bytes[*start..*end] {
+                    if *byte == b'&' {
+                        *byte = b'x';
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        // Reserved JSX attribute names may prevent the opening element from
+        // forming at all. Recognize only an unquoted attribute outside {...}.
+        for c in re!(r"\b(class|default|for)\s*=").captures_iter(text) {
+            let m = c.get(1).unwrap();
+            let p = m.start();
+            if protected[p] {
+                continue;
+            }
+            spend!(p);
+            if let Some(open) = text[..p].rfind('<') {
+                let fragment = &text[open + 1..p];
+                if fragment
+                    .as_bytes()
+                    .first()
+                    .is_some_and(u8::is_ascii_alphabetic)
+                    && !fragment.contains(['<', '>'])
+                {
+                    let mut braces = 0isize;
+                    for i in open + 1..p {
+                        if !protected[i] {
+                            if bytes[i] == b'{' {
+                                braces += 1;
+                            }
+                            if bytes[i] == b'}' {
+                                braces -= 1;
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                    if braces == 0 {
+                        bytes[p + 1] = b'_';
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        let re = re!(r"\b(using|as|abstract|type|delete)\b");
+        for c in re.captures_iter(text) {
+            let m = c.get(1).unwrap();
+            if protected[m.start()] {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let after = text[m.end()..].trim_start();
+            let before = text[..m.start()].trim_end();
+            let identifier_role = match m.as_str() {
+                "using" => after.starts_with([',', ')', '}', ':', '=', ';', '.']),
+                "as" => after.starts_with(['=', ',', ';', ':', '.']),
+                "abstract" => after.starts_with(':') || after.starts_with("?:"),
+                "type" => {
+                    (before.ends_with("typeof") && after.starts_with([')', ']', '.']))
+                        || after.starts_with([',', '}'])
+                            && before
+                                .rsplit_once("export")
+                                .is_some_and(|(_, s)| s.contains('{') && !s.contains('}'))
+                }
+                "delete" => {
+                    before.ends_with("as")
+                        && after.starts_with([',', '}'])
+                        && before
+                            .rsplit_once("export")
+                            .is_some_and(|(_, s)| s.contains('{') && !s.contains('}'))
+                }
+                _ => false,
+            };
+            if identifier_role {
+                bytes[m.start() + 1] = b'_';
+            }
+        }
+        let import_word = re!(r"\bimport\b");
+        for block in re!(r"\b(import|export)\s*\{[^{}]*\}").captures_iter(text) {
+            let m = block.get(0).unwrap();
+            if protected[m.start()] {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let kind = &block[1];
+            for word in import_word.find_iter(&text[m.start() + kind.len()..m.end()]) {
+                let start = m.start() + kind.len() + word.start();
+                let end = m.start() + kind.len() + word.end();
+                if protected[start] {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                let after = text[end..m.end()].trim_start();
+                let before = text[m.start()..start].trim_end();
+                if kind == "import" && after.starts_with("as ")
+                    || kind == "export" && before.ends_with("as")
+                {
+                    bytes[start + 1] = b'_';
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        let mut pending = vec![tree.root_node()];
+        while let Some(n) = pending.pop() {
+            spend!(1usize);
+            if language == "typescript" && n.kind() == "type_alias_declaration" {
+                if let Some(params) = n.child_by_field_name("type_parameters") {
+                    let a = params.start_byte();
+                    let b = params.end_byte();
+                    spend!(b - a);
+                    if b >= a + 2
+                        && text.as_bytes()[a] == b'<'
+                        && text.as_bytes()[b - 1] == b'>'
+                        && trivia_only(&text[a + 1..b - 1])
+                    {
+                        bytes[a] = b' ';
+                        bytes[b - 1] = b' ';
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            if n.kind() == "arrow_function"
+                && text.as_bytes().get(n.end_byte().saturating_sub(1)) == Some(&b'}')
+            {
+                let suffix = &text[n.end_byte()..];
+                let gap = suffix.len() - suffix.trim_start().len();
+                if suffix[..gap].contains('\n') && suffix[gap..].starts_with('[') {
+                    if let Some(p) = (n.end_byte()..n.end_byte() + gap)
+                        .rev()
+                        .find(|p| matches!(bytes[*p], b' ' | b'\t'))
+                    {
+                        bytes[p] = b';';
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            let mut c = n.walk();
+            for child in n.named_children(&mut c) {
+                pending.push(child);
+            }
+        }
+        // Type arguments on tagged templates are parsed as a validated type list.
+        if language == "typescript" {
+            for c in re!(r"([A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_.$]*)<([^<>]+)>[ \t]*`").captures_iter(text) {
+                let whole = c.get(0).unwrap();
+                let args = c.get(2).unwrap();
+                if protected[whole.start()] || args.len() > 4096 {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                let mut p = tree_sitter::Parser::new();
+                p.set_language(&tree_sitter_typescript::LANGUAGE_TYPESCRIPT.into())
+                    .unwrap();
+                let check = format!("type Compatibility = Type<{}>;", args.as_str());
+                if p.parse(&check, None)
+                    .is_some_and(|t| !t.root_node().has_error())
+                {
+                    for b in &mut bytes[args.start() - 1..args.end() + 1] {
+                        if !matches!(*b, b'\n' | b'\r') {
+                            *b = b' ';
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        for c in re!(r"(?m)^([ \t]*)#[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*([ \t]*\r?\n(?:[ \t]*\r?\n)*[ \t]+)\[")
+            .captures_iter(text)
+        {
+            let m = c.get(0).unwrap();
+            let hash = m.start() + c[1].len();
+            if protected[hash] {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let mut n = tree.root_node().descendant_for_byte_range(hash, hash + 1);
+            let mut in_class = false;
+            while let Some(node) = n {
+                spend!(1usize);
+                if node.kind() == "class_body" {
+                    in_class = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                n = node.parent();
+            }
+            if in_class {
+                let gap = c.get(2).unwrap();
+                if let Some(p) = gap
+                    .range()
+                    .rev()
+                    .find(|p| matches!(bytes[*p], b' ' | b'\t'))
+                {
+                    bytes[p] = b';';
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if !module_attributes(text, &protected, &mut bytes, &mut remaining) {
+            return (text.as_bytes().to_vec(), true);
+        }
+        let resource =
+            re!(r"\b(await[ \t]+)?using([ \t]+)([A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*)[ \t]*(?:=|of\b)");
+        for c in resource.captures_iter(text) {
+            let m = c.get(0).unwrap();
+            if protected[m.start()] {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let start = if let Some(a) = c.get(1) {
+                for b in &mut bytes[a.range()] {
+                    *b = b' ';
+                }
+                a.end()
+            } else {
+                m.start()
+            };
+            bytes[start..start + 5].copy_from_slice(b"const");
+        }
+        // Decorator auto-accessors are class fields; erase the modifier only.
+        for m in re!(r"\baccessor[ \t]*(?:[A-Za-z_$#]|\[)").find_iter(text) {
+            if protected[m.start()] {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let mut n = tree
+                .root_node()
+                .descendant_for_byte_range(m.start(), m.end());
+            let mut class_body = false;
+            while let Some(node) = n {
+                spend!(1usize);
+                if node.kind() == "class_body" {
+                    class_body = true;
+                    break;
+                }
+                n = node.parent();
+            }
+            if class_body {
+                bytes[m.start()..m.start() + 8].fill(b' ');
+            }
+        }
+        // Babel/V8 do-expressions are parse-only closures. Require an expression
+        // position and spare whitespace for the opening brace; no bytes move.
+        for m in re!(r"\bdo[ \t]+\{").find_iter(text) {
+            if protected[m.start()] {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let prefix = text[..m.start()].trim_end();
+            if !(prefix.ends_with(['=', '(', ':', '?'])
+                || prefix.ends_with("return")
+                || prefix.ends_with("throw"))
+            {
+                continue;
+            }
+            if m.len() != 4 {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let next = text.as_bytes()[m.end()..]
+                .iter()
+                .position(|b| !b.is_ascii_whitespace())
+                .map(|n| m.end() + n)
+                .unwrap_or(text.len());
+            if let Some(space) = (m.end()..next).find(|i| matches!(bytes[*i], b' ' | b'\t')) {
+                bytes[m.start()..m.end()].copy_from_slice(b"()=>");
+                bytes[space] = b'{';
+            }
+        }
+        // Anonymous ambient default function signatures need a name in the
+        // pinned grammar. Consume existing indentation only, preserving spans.
+        if text.contains("declare module") {
+            for m in
+                re!(r"export[ \t]+default[ \t]+function[ \t]*\([ \t]*\r?\n[ \t]+").find_iter(text)
+            {
+                if protected[m.start()] {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                let open = m.start() + text[m.range()].find('(').unwrap();
+                let space = m.end() - 1;
+                bytes[open] = b'f';
+                bytes[space] = b'(';
+            }
+        }
+        // NUL is permitted within JS/TS quoted/template text, not in identifiers.
+        for (i, b) in text.bytes().enumerate() {
+            if b == 0 && protected[i] {
+                bytes[i] = b' ';
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if matches!(language, "javascript" | "typescript")
+        && !jsx_attributes::normalize(text, &protected, &mut bytes, &mut remaining)
+    {
+        return (text.as_bytes().to_vec(), true);
+    }
+    if language == "typescript"
+        && !typescript_types::normalize(text, tree, &protected, &mut bytes, &mut remaining)
+    {
+        return (text.as_bytes().to_vec(), true);
+    }
+    if language == "rust" {
+        // Syn validates the complete Rust syntax before any attribute/type
+        // compatibility erasure. It never expands macros or runs source code.
+        // A conservative delimiter cap avoids recursive parser exhaustion.
+        let mut depth = 0usize;
+        let mut safe = true;
+        for (i, c) in text.bytes().enumerate() {
+            if protected[i] {
+                continue;
+            }
+            if b"([{ ".contains(&c) && c != b' ' {
+                depth += 1;
+                if depth > 128 {
+                    safe = false;
+                    break;
+                }
+            } else if b")] }".contains(&c) && c != b' ' {
+                depth = depth.saturating_sub(1);
+            }
+        }
+        if safe {
+            let mut pending = vec![(tree.root_node(), 0usize)];
+            while let Some((node, level)) = pending.pop() {
+                spend!(1usize);
+                if level > 128 {
+                    safe = false;
+                    break;
+                }
+                let mut c = node.walk();
+                for child in node.named_children(&mut c) {
+                    pending.push((child, level + 1));
+                }
+            }
+            let mut angle = 0usize;
+            for (i, c) in text.bytes().enumerate() {
+                if protected[i] {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                match c {
+                    b'<' => {
+                        angle += 1;
+                        if angle > 128 {
+                            safe = false;
+                            break;
+                        }
+                    }
+                    b'>' => angle = angle.saturating_sub(1),
+                    b';' | b'{' | b'}' => angle = 0,
+                    _ => {}
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if safe && text.len() <= 2 * 1024 * 1024 && syn::parse_file(text).is_ok() {
+            let raw = text.as_bytes();
+            let mut i = 0;
+            while i + 1 < raw.len() {
+                if raw[i] == b'#'
+                    && !protected[i]
+                    && (raw[i + 1] == b'[' || raw.get(i + 1..i + 3) == Some(b"!["))
+                {
+                    let begin = i;
+                    let open = if raw[i + 1] == b'[' { i + 1 } else { i + 2 };
+                    let mut level = 1usize;
+                    i = open + 1;
+                    while i < raw.len() && level > 0 {
+                        if !protected[i] {
+                            if raw[i] == b'[' {
+                                level += 1;
+                            } else if raw[i] == b']' {
+                                level -= 1;
+                            }
+                        }
+                        i += 1;
+                    }
+                    if level == 0 {
+                        for b in &mut bytes[begin..i] {
+                            if !matches!(*b, b'\n' | b'\r') {
+                                *b = b' ';
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                    continue;
+                }
+                i += 1;
+            }
+            for m in re!(r"\$(?:[ \t]*[\]\)},;])").find_iter(text) {
+                if !protected[m.start()] {
+                    bytes[m.start()] = b'+';
+                }
+            }
+            for c in re!(r":[ \t]*(mut)[ \t]+[A-Za-z_]").captures_iter(text) {
+                let m = c.get(1).unwrap();
+                if !protected[m.start()] {
+                    bytes[m.range()].fill(b' ');
+                }
+            }
+            let mut stack = vec![tree.root_node()];
+            while let Some(n) = stack.pop() {
+                spend!(1usize);
+                if matches!(n.kind(), "attribute_item" | "inner_attribute_item") {
+                    for i in n.byte_range() {
+                        if !matches!(bytes[i], b'\n' | b'\r') {
+                            bytes[i] = b' ';
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                if n.kind() == "macro_definition" {
+                    for i in n.byte_range() {
+                        if !protected[i] && bytes[i] == b'~' {
+                            bytes[i] = b'+';
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+                let mut cursor = n.walk();
+                for child in n.named_children(&mut cursor) {
+                    stack.push(child);
+                }
+            }
+            for c in re!(r"\$([ \t]+)([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)").captures_iter(text) {
+                let m = c.get(0).unwrap();
+                if !protected[m.start()] {
+                    let name = c.get(2).unwrap();
+                    bytes[m.start()] = b' ';
+                    bytes[name.start() - 1] = b'$';
+                }
+            }
+            for m in re!(r#"\bunsafe([ \t]+)extern\s*""#).find_iter(text) {
+                if !protected[m.start()] {
+                    bytes[m.start()..m.start() + 6].fill(b' ');
+                }
+            }
+            for m in
+                re!(r"\bfor\s*<\s*'[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*(?:\s*,\s*'[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*\s*>")
+                    .find_iter(text)
+            {
+                if !protected[m.start()] {
+                    for b in &mut bytes[m.range()] {
+                        if !matches!(*b, b'\n' | b'\r') {
+                            *b = b' ';
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            for m in re!(r"::[ \t]*<[^<>\n]*>").find_iter(text) {
+                if !protected[m.start()] {
+                    bytes[m.range()].fill(b' ');
+                }
+            }
+            for c in
+                re!(r"\bstruct[ \t]+[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*(\s+where[^;{}]+);").captures_iter(text)
+            {
+                let whole = c.get(0).unwrap();
+                if !protected[whole.start()] {
+                    for b in &mut bytes[c.get(1).unwrap().range()] {
+                        if !matches!(*b, b'\n' | b'\r') {
+                            *b = b' ';
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        for m in re!(r"&raw\b").find_iter(text) {
+            let next = text[m.end()..].trim_start();
+            if !protected[m.start()] && !next.starts_with("const ") && !next.starts_with("mut ") {
+                bytes[m.start() + 2] = b'_';
+            }
+        }
+        for m in re!(r"\btry!").find_iter(text) {
+            if !protected[m.start()] {
+                bytes[m.start() + 2] = b'_';
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    if language == "ruby" {
+        let quoted_raise = re!(r#"(?m)^[ \t]*def[ \t]+[^\n=]+[ \t]*=[ \t]*raise([ \t]+)['"]"#);
+        for c in quoted_raise.captures_iter(text) {
+            let m = c.get(0).unwrap();
+            if !protected[m.start()] {
+                bytes[c.get(1).unwrap().start()] = b'+';
+            }
+        }
+        let re = re!(r"(?m)^\s*def\s+[^\n=]+\s*=\s*raise([ \t]+)([A-Z][A-Za-z0-9_:]*)(,)?");
+        for c in re.captures_iter(text) {
+            let m = c.get(0).unwrap();
+            if protected[m.start()] {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let space = c.get(1).unwrap();
+            bytes[space.start()] = b'+';
+            if let Some(comma) = c.get(3) {
+                bytes[comma.start()] = b'+';
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    (bytes, false)
+}
+
+fn trivia_only(text: &str) -> bool {
+    static RE: std::sync::OnceLock<regex::Regex> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
+    RE.get_or_init(|| regex::Regex::new(r"(?s)^(?:\s|/\*.*?\*/|//[^\r\n]*(?:\r?\n|$))*$").unwrap())
+        .is_match(text)
+}
+
+fn module_attributes(
+    text: &str,
+    protected: &[bool],
+    bytes: &mut [u8],
+    remaining: &mut usize,
+) -> bool {
+    let attributes = re!(
+        r#"\b(?:with|assert)\s*\{\s*(?:(?:[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*|'[A-Za-z0-9_$-]+'|"[A-Za-z0-9_$-]+")\s*:\s*(?:'[A-Za-z0-9_./:-]+'|"[A-Za-z0-9_./:-]+")\s*,?\s*)+\}"#
+    );
+    let declaration =
+        re!(r#"(?s)\b(?:import|export)\b[^;{}]*\bfrom\s*(?:'[^'\r\n]*'|"[^"\r\n]*")\s*$"#);
+
+    let ambient = re!(r#"\bdeclare\s+module\s*(?:'[^'\r\n]*'|"[^"\r\n]*")\s*$"#);
+    let field = re!(
+        r#"([A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*|'[A-Za-z0-9_$-]+'|"[A-Za-z0-9_$-]+")\s*:\s*(?:'[A-Za-z0-9_./:-]+'|"[A-Za-z0-9_./:-]+")"#
+    );
+    for m in attributes.find_iter(text) {
+        let Some(rest) = remaining.checked_sub(m.start() + m.len()) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        *remaining = rest;
+        if protected[m.start()] {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let Some(d) = declaration
+            .find(&text[..m.start()])
+            .or_else(|| ambient.find(&text[..m.start()]))
+        else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        if protected[d.start()] {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+        let body = &m.as_str()[m.as_str().find('{').unwrap() + 1..m.as_str().len() - 1];
+
+        let mut cursor = 0;
+        let mut valid = true;
+        for (index, c) in field.captures_iter(body).enumerate() {
+            let f = c.get(0).unwrap();
+            if body[cursor..f.start()].trim() != if index == 0 { "" } else { "," }
+                || !seen.insert(c[1].trim_matches(['\'', '"']).to_string())
+            {
+                valid = false;
+                break;
+            }
+            cursor = f.end();
+        }
+        if !valid || seen.is_empty() || !["", ","].contains(&body[cursor..].trim()) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        for b in &mut bytes[m.range()] {
+            if !matches!(*b, b'\n' | b'\r') {
+                *b = b' ';
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    true
+}
+
+// One linear pass; no parsing or evaluation of Go expressions.
+fn go_lexical_protection(source: &[u8]) -> Vec<bool> {
+    let mut protected = vec![false; source.len()];
+    let mut i = 0;
+    while i < source.len() {
+        let start = i;
+        if source[i..].starts_with(b"//") {
+            i += 2;
+            while i < source.len() && source[i] != b'\n' {
+                i += 1;
+            }
+        } else if source[i..].starts_with(b"/*") {
+            i += 2;
+            while i < source.len() && !source[i..].starts_with(b"*/") {
+                i += 1;
+            }
+            i = (i + 2).min(source.len());
+        } else if matches!(source[i], b'"' | b'\'' | b'`') {
+            let quote = source[i];
+            i += 1;
+            while i < source.len() {
+                let b = source[i];
+                i += 1;
+                if quote != b'`' && b == b'\\' {
+                    i = (i + 1).min(source.len());
+                } else if b == quote {
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+        } else {
+            i += 1;
+            continue;
+        }
+        protected[start..i].fill(true);
+    }
+    protected
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {
+    #[test]
+    fn go_lexical_boundaries_protect_literals_and_comments_beside_expression_new() {
+        let source = "var raw = `new(1)`; var quoted = \"new(2)\\\"still quoted\"; var rune = '('; // new(3)\n/* new(4) */ var live = new(\"a\")";
+        let mask = super::go_lexical_protection(source.as_bytes());
+        for literal in [
+            "new(1)",
+            "new(2)",
+            "new(3)",
+            "new(4)",
+            "still quoted",
+            "'('",
+        ] {
+            let start = source.find(literal).unwrap();
+            assert!(
+                mask[start..start + literal.len()].iter().all(|v| *v),
+                "{literal}"
+            );
+        }
+        let live = source.find("new(\"a\")").unwrap();
+        assert!(mask[live..live + 4].iter().all(|v| !*v));
+        assert!(mask[live + 4..live + 7].iter().all(|v| *v));
+    }
+}

index 0000000..1d54a55

+//! Offset-preserving compatibility for supported script syntax.
+use super::javascript_lexer::{identifier, spend, tokens, Token};
+use tree_sitter::Tree;
+fn blank(bytes: &mut [u8], a: usize, b: usize) {
+    for c in &mut bytes[a..b] {
+        if !matches!(*c, b'\n' | b'\r') {
+            *c = b' ';
+        }
+    }
+}
+fn closing(
+    ts: &[Token<'_>],
+    start: usize,
+    open: &str,
+    close: &str,
+    left: &mut usize,
+) -> Option<usize> {
+    let mut depth = 0;
+    for (i, t) in ts.iter().enumerate().skip(start) {
+        spend(left, 1)?;
+        if t.value == open {
+            depth += 1;
+        }
+        if t.value == close {
+            depth -= 1;
+            if depth == 0 {
+                return Some(i);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    None
+}
+fn strict_directive(ts: &[Token<'_>]) -> bool {
+    for t in ts {
+        if t.value == ";" {
+            continue;
+        }
+        if !t.value.starts_with(['\'', '"']) {
+            break;
+        }
+        if matches!(t.value, "'use strict'" | "\"use strict\"") {
+            return true;
+        }
+    }
+    false
+}
+pub(super) fn normalize(
+    text: &str,
+    tree: &Tree,
+    bytes: &mut [u8],
+    left: &mut usize,
+    module: bool,
+) -> bool {
+    let Some(ts) = tokens(text, tree, bytes, left) else {
+        return false;
+    };
+    let mut depth = 0;
+    let mut strict = module
+        || strict_directive(&ts)
+        || text
+            .lines()
+            .take(10)
+            .any(|l| l.trim_start().starts_with("// Flags:") && l.contains("--use-strict"));
+    for (i, t) in ts.iter().enumerate() {
+        if depth == 0
+            && (t.value == "export"
+                || t.value == "import"
+                    && ts.get(i + 1).is_some_and(|n| !matches!(n.value, "(" | ".")))
+        {
+            strict = true;
+        }
+        if t.value == "{" {
+            depth += 1;
+        }
+        if t.value == "}" {
+            depth -= 1;
+        }
+    }
+    let mut functions = vec![];
+    let mut classes = vec![];
+    for (i, t) in ts.iter().enumerate() {
+        if spend(left, 1).is_none() {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if t.value == "class" {
+            let mut opening = None;
+            for (j, t) in ts.iter().enumerate().skip(i + 1) {
+                if spend(left, 1).is_none() {
+                    return false;
+                }
+                if t.value == "{" {
+                    opening = Some(j);
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+            if let Some(a) = opening {
+                if let Some(b) = closing(&ts, a, "{", "}", left) {
+                    classes.push((ts[a].start, ts[b].end));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if t.value != "function" {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let mut a = i + 1;
+        let generator = ts.get(a).is_some_and(|t| t.value == "*");
+        if generator {
+            a += 1;
+        }
+        if ts.get(a).is_some_and(|t| t.value != "(") {
+            a += 1;
+        }
+        if ts.get(a).is_none_or(|t| t.value != "(") {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let Some(b) = closing(&ts, a, "(", ")", left) else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        if ts.get(b + 1).is_none_or(|t| t.value != "{") {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let Some(end) = closing(&ts, b + 1, "{", "}", left) else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        let own_strict = strict_directive(&ts[b + 2..end]);
+        functions.push((ts[i].start, ts[end].end, generator, own_strict));
+    }
+    let mut block_ends = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut nodes = vec![tree.root_node()];
+    while let Some(n) = nodes.pop() {
+        if spend(left, 1).is_none() {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if n.kind() == "method_definition" {
+            if let Some(body) = n.child_by_field_name("body") {
+                let mut c = n.walk();
+                let generator = n.children(&mut c).any(|c| c.kind() == "*");
+                let start = ts.partition_point(|t| t.start <= body.start_byte());
+                let end = ts.partition_point(|t| t.start < body.end_byte());
+                functions.push((
+                    n.start_byte(),
+                    n.end_byte(),
+                    generator,
+                    strict_directive(&ts[start..end]),
+                ));
+            }
+        }
+        if n.kind() == "statement_block"
+            && n.parent().is_some_and(|p| {
+                matches!(
+                    p.kind(),
+                    "catch_clause"
+                        | "try_statement"
+                        | "if_statement"
+                        | "for_statement"
+                        | "for_in_statement"
+                        | "while_statement"
+                        | "function_declaration"
+                        | "generator_function_declaration"
+                        | "program"
+                        | "statement_block"
+                )
+            })
+        {
+            block_ends.insert(n.end_byte());
+        }
+        let mut c = n.walk();
+        nodes.extend(n.named_children(&mut c));
+    }
+    let mut control_ends = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+    for (i, t) in ts.iter().enumerate() {
+        if matches!(
+            t.value,
+            "if" | "while" | "for" | "with" | "catch" | "switch"
+        ) && ts.get(i + 1).is_some_and(|t| t.value == "(")
+        {
+            if let Some(end) = closing(&ts, i + 1, "(", ")", left) {
+                control_ends.insert(end);
+                if ts.get(end + 1).is_some_and(|t| t.value == "{") {
+                    if let Some(close) = closing(&ts, end + 1, "{", "}", left) {
+                        block_ends.insert(ts[close].end);
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (i, t) in ts.iter().enumerate() {
+        if matches!(t.value, "else" | "try" | "finally" | "do")
+            && ts.get(i + 1).is_some_and(|t| t.value == "{")
+        {
+            if let Some(close) = closing(&ts, i + 1, "{", "}", left) {
+                block_ends.insert(ts[close].end);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (i, t) in ts.iter().enumerate() {
+        if spend(left, functions.len() + classes.len() + 1).is_none() {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if t.value == "yield" && !strict {
+            let scope = functions
+                .iter()
+                .filter(|f| f.0 <= t.start && t.end <= f.1)
+                .min_by_key(|f| f.1 - f.0);
+            let forbidden = scope.is_some_and(|f| f.2)
+                || functions
+                    .iter()
+                    .any(|f| f.0 <= t.start && t.end <= f.1 && f.3)
+                || classes.iter().any(|f| f.0 <= t.start && t.end <= f.1);
+            if !forbidden {
+                bytes[t.start + 1] = b'_';
+            }
+        }
+        if t.value == "%"
+            && ts
+                .get(i + 1)
+                .is_some_and(|n| n.value.as_bytes().first().is_some_and(|b| identifier(*b)))
+            && ts.get(i + 2).is_some_and(|n| n.value == "(")
+        {
+            let previous = i.checked_sub(1).and_then(|j| ts.get(j));
+            if previous.is_some_and(|p| p.value == "}" && block_ends.contains(&p.end))
+                || i.checked_sub(1).is_some_and(|j| control_ends.contains(&j))
+                || previous.is_none_or(|p| {
+                    matches!(
+                        p.value,
+                        "(" | "["
+                            | "{"
+                            | "="
+                            | ":"
+                            | ","
+                            | ";"
+                            | "!"
+                            | "?"
+                            | "&&"
+                            | "||"
+                            | "return"
+                            | "throw"
+                            | "typeof"
+                            | "void"
+                            | "delete"
+                            | "in"
+                            | "of"
+                            | "else"
+                            | "+"
+                            | "-"
+                            | "*"
+                            | "/"
+                            | "**"
+                            | "=="
+                            | "==="
+                            | "!="
+                            | "!=="
+                            | "<"
+                            | ">"
+                            | "<="
+                            | ">="
+                            | "~"
+                            | "&"
+                            | "|"
+                            | "^"
+                            | "=>"
+                    )
+                })
+            {
+                bytes[t.start] = b' ';
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    for (i, t) in ts.iter().enumerate() {
+        if t.value != "[" {
+            continue;
+        }
+        if i > 0
+            && !matches!(
+                ts[i - 1].value,
+                ";" | "{" | "}" | "(" | "=" | "," | "return"
+            )
+        {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let Some(end) = closing(&ts, i, "[", "]", left) else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        if ts.get(end + 1).is_none_or(|t| t.value != "=") {
+            continue;
+        }
+        // Only direct pattern elements qualify. Computed class fields may also
+        // have [...] = initializers, with arbitrary nested function bodies.
+        let mut depth = 0usize;
+        for j in i + 1..end {
+            if spend(left, 1).is_none() {
+                return false;
+            }
+            if depth == 0
+                && ts[j].value == "("
+                && ts
+                    .get(j + 1)
+                    .is_some_and(|t| t.value.bytes().all(identifier))
+                && ts.get(j + 2).is_some_and(|t| t.value == ")")
+                && ts
+                    .get(j + 3)
+                    .is_some_and(|t| matches!(t.value, "," | "]" | "="))
+            {
+                bytes[ts[j].start] = b' ';
+                bytes[ts[j + 2].start] = b' ';
+            }
+            match ts[j].value {
+                "(" | "[" | "{" => depth += 1,
+                ")" | "]" | "}" => depth = depth.saturating_sub(1),
+                _ => {}
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    // Call expressions as assignment/for-in targets are legacy runtime errors,
+    // not successful assignable references. Never touch optional-chain targets.
+    let mut pending = vec![tree.root_node()];
+    while let Some(n) = pending.pop() {
+        if spend(left, 1).is_none() {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if n.kind() == "call_expression" && !n.has_error() && !text[n.byte_range()].contains("?.") {
+            let after = text[n.end_byte()..].trim_start();
+            if (after.starts_with('=') && !after.starts_with("==") && !after.starts_with("=>"))
+                || after.starts_with("**=")
+                || after.starts_with("in ")
+            {
+                blank(bytes, n.start_byte(), n.end_byte());
+                bytes[n.start_byte()] = b'_';
+            }
+        }
+        if n.kind() == "parenthesized_expression"
+            && n.named_child_count() == 1
+            && n.named_child(0).is_some_and(|c| c.kind() == "identifier")
+            && n.parent()
+                .is_some_and(|p| matches!(p.kind(), "array_pattern" | "assignment_pattern"))
+        {
+            bytes[n.start_byte()] = b' ';
+            bytes[n.end_byte() - 1] = b' ';
+        }
+        let mut c = n.walk();
+        for ch in n.named_children(&mut c) {
+            pending.push(ch);
+        }
+    }
+    *left > 0
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {
+    #[test]
+    fn literal_regex_and_remainder_percent_bytes_are_preserved() {
+        for text in [
+            "const x = value %Native(); const s='%Native(yield)';",
+            r"if (x) /%Native\(yield\)/.test(s);",
+            "const x = function() {} %Native();",
+            "var yield; yield %Native();",
+            "const x = class {} %Native();",
+        ] {
+            let mut parser = tree_sitter::Parser::new();
+            parser
+                .set_language(&tree_sitter_javascript::LANGUAGE.into())
+                .unwrap();
+            let tree = parser.parse(text, None).unwrap();
+            let mut bytes = text.as_bytes().to_vec();
+            assert!(super::normalize(
+                text,
+                &tree,
+                &mut bytes,
+                &mut 1000000,
+                false
+            ));
+            for (offset, _) in text.match_indices('%') {
+                assert_eq!(bytes[offset], b'%', "{text}");
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    #[test]
+    fn computed_field_nested_calls_and_catch_keep_their_parentheses() {
+        let text = "class C { [(exported = function access(r,v){try {r.m()} catch(e){} advance(v);}, 'x')] = 1; }";
+        let mut parser = tree_sitter::Parser::new();
+        parser
+            .set_language(&tree_sitter_javascript::LANGUAGE.into())
+            .unwrap();
+        let tree = parser.parse(text, None).unwrap();
+        let mut bytes = text.as_bytes().to_vec();
+        assert!(super::normalize(
+            text,
+            &tree,
+            &mut bytes,
+            &mut 1_000_000,
+            false
+        ));
+        assert_eq!(bytes, text.as_bytes());
+    }
+    #[test]
+    fn depleted_compatibility_work_is_reported_as_incomplete() {
+        let text = "function f(yield){yield()}";
+        let mut parser = tree_sitter::Parser::new();
+        parser
+            .set_language(&tree_sitter_javascript::LANGUAGE.into())
+            .unwrap();
+        let tree = parser.parse(text, None).unwrap();
+        assert!(!super::normalize(
+            text,
+            &tree,
+            &mut text.as_bytes().to_vec(),
+            &mut 1,
+            false
+        ));
+    }
+}

+//! Script grammar compatibility. Original source remains the evidence authority.
+use tree_sitter::Tree;
+#[derive(Clone)]
+pub(super) struct Token<'a> {
+    pub(super) value: &'a str,
+    pub(super) start: usize,
+    pub(super) end: usize,
+}
+pub(super) fn spend(left: &mut usize, cost: usize) -> Option<()> {
+    *left = left.checked_sub(cost)?;
+    Some(())
+}
+pub(super) fn identifier(b: u8) -> bool {
+    b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(b, b'_' | b'$')
+}
+// Quotes/comments are recognized without trusting an error-recovery tree.
+pub(super) fn tokens<'a>(
+    text: &'a str,
+    tree: &Tree,
+    bytes: &mut [u8],
+    left: &mut usize,
+) -> Option<Vec<Token<'a>>> {
+    // Invalid raw escapes can corrupt the original recovery tree, obscuring the
+    // tag itself. Use an escape-neutral discovery tree only to prove tag roles;
+    // the final view still changes escapes exclusively in proven tagged text.
+    let mut probe = text.as_bytes().to_vec();
+    tokens_inner(text, tree, &mut probe, left, true)?;
+    if probe == text.as_bytes() {
+        return tokens_inner(text, tree, bytes, left, false);
+    }
+    spend(left, text.len().saturating_mul(4))?;
+    let mut parser = tree_sitter::Parser::new();
+    parser
+        .set_language(&tree_sitter_javascript::LANGUAGE.into())
+        .ok()?;
+    let discovery = parser.parse(&probe, None)?;
+    tokens_inner(text, &discovery, bytes, left, false)
+}
+fn tokens_inner<'a>(
+    text: &'a str,
+    tree: &Tree,
+    bytes: &mut [u8],
+    left: &mut usize,
+    discover_tags: bool,
+) -> Option<Vec<Token<'a>>> {
+    let mut regexes = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
+    let mut tags = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut nodes = vec![tree.root_node()];
+    while let Some(n) = nodes.pop() {
+        spend(left, 1)?;
+        if n.kind() == "call_expression"
+            && n.child_by_field_name("function")
+                .is_some_and(|f| !f.is_error())
+        {
+            if let Some(arg) = n
+                .child_by_field_name("arguments")
+                .filter(|a| a.kind() == "template_string")
+            {
+                tags.insert(arg.start_byte());
+            }
+        }
+        if n.kind() == "regex" {
+            regexes.insert(n.start_byte(), n.end_byte());
+        }
+        let mut c = n.walk();
+        nodes.extend(n.named_children(&mut c));
+    }
+    let s = text.as_bytes();
+    let mut i = 0;
+    let mut out: Vec<Token<'a>> = vec![];
+    while i < s.len() {
+        spend(left, 1)?;
+        let a = i;
+        if let Some(end) = regexes.get(&i) {
+            i = *end;
+            out.push(Token {
+                value: "regex",
+                start: a,
+                end: i,
+            });
+            continue;
+        }
+        if s[i].is_ascii_whitespace()
+            || text[i..].chars().next().is_some_and(|c| {
+                matches!(
+                    c,
+                    '\u{00a0}' | '\u{feff}' | '\u{1680}' | '\u{2000}'
+                        ..='\u{200a}'
+                            | '\u{2028}'
+                            | '\u{2029}'
+                            | '\u{202f}'
+                            | '\u{205f}'
+                            | '\u{3000}'
+                )
+            })
+        {
+            i += text[i..].chars().next()?.len_utf8();
+            continue;
+        }
+        if s[i..].starts_with(b"//") {
+            while i < s.len() && s[i] != b'\n' {
+                i += 1;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+        if s[i..].starts_with(b"/*") {
+            i += 2;
+            while i < s.len() && !s[i..].starts_with(b"*/") {
+                i += 1;
+            }
+            i = (i + 2).min(s.len());
+            continue;
+        }
+        if s[i] == b'/'
+            && out.last().is_none_or(|p| {
+                matches!(
+                    p.value,
+                    "=" | "(" | "[" | "{" | "," | ":" | ";" | "return" | "throw" | "=>" | "case"
+                )
+            })
+        {
+            i += 1;
+            let mut class = false;
+            while i < s.len() {
+                let c = s[i];
+                i += 1;
+                if c == b'\\' {
+                    i = (i + 1).min(s.len());
+                } else if c == b'[' {
+                    class = true;
+                } else if c == b']' {
+                    class = false;
+                } else if c == b'/' && !class || c == b'\n' {
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+            while i < s.len() && s[i].is_ascii_alphabetic() {
+                i += 1;
+            }
+            out.push(Token {
+                value: "regex",
+                start: a,
+                end: i,
+            });
+            continue;
+        }
+        if matches!(s[i], b'\'' | b'"') {
+            let q = s[i];
+            i += 1;
+            while i < s.len() {
+                let c = s[i];
+                i += 1;
+                if c == b'\\' {
+                    i = (i + 1).min(s.len());
+                } else if c == q {
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+        } else if s[i] == b'`' {
+            let tagged = discover_tags || tags.contains(&a);
+            i += 1;
+            let mut interpolation = 0usize;
+            while i < s.len() {
+                spend(left, 1)?;
+                if interpolation > 0 && regexes.contains_key(&i) {
+                    i = regexes[&i];
+                    continue;
+                }
+                if interpolation > 0 && s[i..].starts_with(b"//") {
+                    while i < s.len() && s[i] != b'\n' {
+                        i += 1;
+                    }
+                    continue;
+                }
+                if interpolation > 0 && s[i..].starts_with(b"/*") {
+                    i += 2;
+                    while i < s.len() && !s[i..].starts_with(b"*/") {
+                        i += 1;
+                    }
+                    i = (i + 2).min(s.len());
+                    continue;
+                }
+                if s[i] == b'\\' {
+                    if tagged
+                        && interpolation == 0
+                        && !s
+                            .get(i + 1)
+                            .is_some_and(|c| matches!(*c, b'`' | b'\\' | b'$'))
+                    {
+                        bytes[i] = b'x';
+                    }
+                    i = (i + 2).min(s.len());
+                } else if interpolation > 0 && matches!(s[i], b'\'' | b'"') {
+                    let q = s[i];
+                    i += 1;
+                    while i < s.len() {
+                        let c = s[i];
+                        i += 1;
+                        if c == b'\\' {
+                            i = (i + 1).min(s.len());
+                        } else if c == q {
+                            break;
+                        }
+                    }
+                } else if s[i] == b'`' && interpolation == 0 {
+                    i += 1;
+                    break;
+                } else if interpolation > 0 && s[i] == b'{' {
+                    interpolation += 1;
+                    i += 1;
+                } else if interpolation > 0 && s[i] == b'`' {
+                    // Nested template contents are left untouched. Incomplete
+                    // nested syntax cannot be repaired by the outer tag.
+                    i += 1;
+                    while i < s.len() {
+                        let c = s[i];
+                        i += 1;
+                        if c == b'\\' {
+                            i = (i + 1).min(s.len());
+                        } else if c == b'`' {
+                            break;
+                        }
+                    }
+                } else if s[i..].starts_with(b"${") {
+                    interpolation += 1;
+                    i += 2;
+                } else if s[i] == b'}' && interpolation > 0 {
+                    interpolation -= 1;
+                    i += 1;
+                } else {
+                    i += 1;
+                }
+            }
+            out.push(Token {
+                value: "template",
+                start: a,
+                end: i,
+            });
+            continue;
+        } else if identifier(s[i]) {
+            i += 1;
+            while i < s.len() && identifier(s[i]) {
+                i += 1;
+            }
+        } else {
+            i += text[a..].chars().next()?.len_utf8();
+            for op in [
+                "**=", "===", "!==", "=>", "==", "!=", "++", "--", "&&", "||", "?.", "**",
+            ] {
+                if text[a..].starts_with(op) {
+                    i = a + op.len();
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if let Some(value) = text.get(a..i) {
+            out.push(Token {
+                value,
+                start: a,
+                end: i,
+            });
+        }
+    }
+    Some(out)
+}

+//! Recover quoted JSX attribute values even when entity recovery loses the tag.
+//! Only ampersands in complete quoted attributes of a closed opening tag change.
+fn name_byte(b: u8) -> bool {
+    b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"_$:.-".contains(&b)
+}
+fn quoted(source: &[u8], i: &mut usize, escape: bool) -> bool {
+    let quote = source[*i];
+    *i += 1;
+    while *i < source.len() {
+        let b = source[*i];
+        *i += 1;
+        if b == quote {
+            return true;
+        }
+        if escape && b == b'\\' {
+            *i = (*i + 1).min(source.len());
+        }
+    }
+    false
+}
+fn braced(source: &[u8], i: &mut usize) -> bool {
+    let mut depth = 0;
+    while *i < source.len() {
+        match source[*i] {
+            b'\'' | b'"' | b'`' => {
+                if !quoted(source, i, true) {
+                    return false;
+                }
+            }
+            b'/' if source[*i..].starts_with(b"//") => {
+                while *i < source.len() && source[*i] != b'\n' {
+                    *i += 1;
+                }
+            }
+            b'/' if source[*i..].starts_with(b"/*") => {
+                *i += 2;
+                while *i < source.len() && !source[*i..].starts_with(b"*/") {
+                    *i += 1;
+                }
+                if *i == source.len() {
+                    return false;
+                }
+                *i += 2;
+            }
+            b'{' => {
+                depth += 1;
+                *i += 1;
+            }
+            b'}' => {
+                depth -= 1;
+                *i += 1;
+                if depth == 0 {
+                    return true;
+                }
+            }
+            _ => *i += 1,
+        }
+    }
+    false
+}
+fn tag(source: &[u8], start: usize, i: &mut usize, amps: &mut Vec<usize>) -> bool {
+    *i = start + 1;
+    if !source
+        .get(*i)
+        .is_some_and(|b| b.is_ascii_alphabetic() || matches!(*b, b'_' | b'$'))
+    {
+        return false;
+    }
+    while source.get(*i).is_some_and(|b| name_byte(*b)) {
+        *i += 1;
+    }
+    loop {
+        let before = *i;
+        while source.get(*i).is_some_and(u8::is_ascii_whitespace) {
+            *i += 1;
+        }
+        if source.get(*i) == Some(&b'>') {
+            *i += 1;
+            return true;
+        }
+        if source.get(*i..*i + 2) == Some(b"/>") {
+            *i += 2;
+            return true;
+        }
+        if *i == before {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if source.get(*i) == Some(&b'{') {
+            if !braced(source, i) {
+                return false;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+        let begin = *i;
+        while source.get(*i).is_some_and(|b| name_byte(*b)) {
+            *i += 1;
+        }
+        if *i == begin {
+            return false;
+        }
+        let name_end = *i;
+        while source.get(*i).is_some_and(u8::is_ascii_whitespace) {
+            *i += 1;
+        }
+        if source.get(*i) != Some(&b'=') {
+            *i = name_end;
+            continue;
+        }
+        *i += 1;
+        while source.get(*i).is_some_and(u8::is_ascii_whitespace) {
+            *i += 1;
+        }
+        if source.get(*i) == Some(&b'{') {
+            if !braced(source, i) {
+                return false;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+        if !source.get(*i).is_some_and(|b| matches!(*b, b'\'' | b'"')) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        let content = *i + 1;
+        if !quoted(source, i, false) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        for (offset, b) in source[content..*i - 1].iter().enumerate() {
+            if *b == b'&' {
+                amps.push(content + offset);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+pub(super) fn normalize(
+    text: &str,
+    protected: &[bool],
+    bytes: &mut [u8],
+    remaining: &mut usize,
+) -> bool {
+    if !text.contains('&') {
+        return true;
+    }
+    for (start, _) in text.match_indices('<') {
+        if protected[start] {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let mut i = start;
+        let mut amps = Vec::new();
+        let valid = tag(text.as_bytes(), start, &mut i, &mut amps);
+        let Some(rest) = remaining.checked_sub(i.saturating_sub(start)) else {
+            return false;
+        };
+        *remaining = rest;
+        if valid {
+            for offset in amps {
+                bytes[offset] = b'x';
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    true
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {
+    #[test]
+    fn only_complete_quoted_attributes_change_and_work_exhaustion_is_reported() {
+        let text = "<A href='/start?first-key=1&second-key=2' other={x && y} />";
+        let mut bytes = text.as_bytes().to_vec();
+        assert!(super::normalize(
+            text,
+            &vec![false; text.len()],
+            &mut bytes,
+            &mut 10000
+        ));
+        let expected = text.replace("1&second", "1xsecond");
+        assert_eq!(bytes, expected.as_bytes());
+        let mut bytes = text.as_bytes().to_vec();
+        assert!(!super::normalize(
+            text,
+            &vec![false; text.len()],
+            &mut bytes,
+            &mut 1
+        ));
+        for text in ["<A href='a&b' other={ />", "<A href='a&b />"] {
+            let mut bytes = text.as_bytes().to_vec();
+            assert!(super::normalize(
+                text,
+                &vec![false; text.len()],
+                &mut bytes,
+                &mut 10000
+            ));
+            assert_eq!(bytes, text.as_bytes());
+        }
+    }
+}

+//! Bounded, parse-only recognition of import types when the first tree recovers
+//! them as expressions. The probe tree never supplies evidence or output bytes.
+use tree_sitter::{Node, Parser, Tree};
+
+fn charge(remaining: &mut usize, cost: usize) -> bool {
+    if let Some(next) = remaining.checked_sub(cost) {
+        *remaining = next;
+        true
+    } else {
+        false
+    }
+}
+fn adapt(bytes: &mut [u8], start: usize, open: usize, end: usize, qualified_end: usize) {
+    bytes[start + 1] = b'_';
+    bytes[open] = b'<';
+    bytes[end - 1] = b'>';
+    for b in &mut bytes[end..qualified_end] {
+        if *b == b'.' {
+            *b = b'&';
+        }
+    }
+}
+fn type_context(
+    mut node: Node<'_>,
+    start: usize,
+    end: usize,
+    remaining: &mut usize,
+) -> Option<bool> {
+    loop {
+        if !charge(remaining, 1) {
+            return None;
+        }
+        match node.kind() {
+            "type_query"
+            | "type_annotation"
+            | "type_arguments"
+            | "type_alias_declaration"
+            | "generic_type"
+            | "type_parameter"
+            | "function_type"
+            | "constructor_type" => return Some(true),
+            "as_expression" | "satisfies_expression" => {
+                let mut cursor = node.walk();
+                for child in node.children(&mut cursor) {
+                    if !charge(remaining, 1) {
+                        return None;
+                    }
+                    if matches!(child.kind(), "as" | "satisfies") && child.end_byte() <= start {
+                        return Some(true);
+                    }
+                }
+                return Some(false);
+            }
+            // A containing type declaration does not turn its runtime expressions
+            // (e.g. computed property names) into import types.
+            "arrow_function"
+            | "statement_block"
+            | "computed_property_name"
+            | "arguments"
+            | "expression_statement"
+            | "pair"
+            | "object" => return Some(false),
+            _ => {}
+        }
+        let Some(parent) = node.parent() else {
+            return Some(false);
+        };
+        if parent.start_byte() > start || parent.end_byte() < end {
+            return Some(false);
+        }
+        node = parent;
+    }
+}
+
+pub(super) fn normalize(
+    text: &str,
+    tree: &Tree,
+    protected: &[bool],
+    bytes: &mut [u8],
+    remaining: &mut usize,
+) -> bool {
+    static IMPORT: std::sync::OnceLock<regex::Regex> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
+    let import = IMPORT.get_or_init(|| {
+        regex::Regex::new(r#"\bimport\s*\(\s*(?:'[^'"\r\n]+'|"[^'"\r\n]+")\s*\)"#).unwrap()
+    });
+    let mut candidates = Vec::new();
+    for m in import.find_iter(text) {
+        if !charge(remaining, m.len()) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        if protected[m.start()] {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let mut qualified_end = m.end();
+        while text.as_bytes().get(qualified_end) == Some(&b'.') {
+            let begin = qualified_end + 1;
+            if !text
+                .as_bytes()
+                .get(begin)
+                .is_some_and(|b| b.is_ascii_alphabetic() || matches!(*b, b'_' | b'$'))
+            {
+                break;
+            }
+            let mut end = begin;
+            while text
+                .as_bytes()
+                .get(end)
+                .is_some_and(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(*b, b'_' | b'$'))
+            {
+                end += 1;
+            }
+            if !charge(remaining, end - qualified_end) {
+                return false;
+            }
+            if end == begin {
+                break;
+            }
+            qualified_end = end;
+        }
+        candidates.push((
+            m.start(),
+            m.start() + m.as_str().find('(').unwrap(),
+            m.end(),
+            qualified_end,
+        ));
+    }
+    if candidates.is_empty() {
+        return true;
+    }
+    if !charge(remaining, bytes.len()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    let mut probe = bytes.to_vec();
+    for &(start, open, end, last) in &candidates {
+        adapt(&mut probe, start, open, end, last);
+    }
+    let mut parser = Parser::new();
+    if parser.set_language(&tree.language()).is_err() {
+        return true;
+    }
+    let Some(probe_tree) = parser.parse(&probe, None) else {
+        return true;
+    };
+    for (start, open, end, last) in candidates {
+        let Some(node) = probe_tree.root_node().descendant_for_byte_range(start, end) else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        match type_context(node, start, end, remaining) {
+            Some(true) => adapt(bytes, start, open, end, last),
+            Some(false) => {}
+            None => return false,
+        }
+    }
+    true
+}
+
+#[cfg(test)]
+mod tests {

+    fn runtime_import_bytes_remain_original_beside_recovered_type_imports() {
+        let text = "type View = () => import('types').Node[];\nconst load = () => import('runtime').then(f);\nasync function run() { await import('runtime2'); }\nconst x = (import('runtime3')) as unknown;";
+        let mut parser = tree_sitter::Parser::new();
+        parser
+            .set_language(&tree_sitter_typescript::LANGUAGE_TYPESCRIPT.into())
+            .unwrap();

+            &text.as_bytes()[..text.find('\n').unwrap()]
+        );
+        let start = text.find('\n').unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(&bytes[start..], &text.as_bytes()[start..]);
+    }

+use pqc_scan_core::{Detector, DetectorStatus, ScannableFile};
+use pqc_scan_detectors::{
+    dependency_detector::DependencyDetector, regex_detector::RegexDetector,
+    tree_sitter_detector::TreeSitterDetector,
+};
+use pqc_scan_rules::RuleSet;
+use std::path::Path;
+fn rules() -> RuleSet {
+    RuleSet::load_from_dir(&Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../rules")).unwrap()
+}
+fn ast(name: &str, text: &str) -> pqc_scan_core::DetectorOutcome {
+    TreeSitterDetector
+        .detect_observed(
+            &ScannableFile::from_bytes(name.into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+            &rules(),
+        )
+        .unwrap()
+}
+fn complete_with_original_hit(name: &str, text: &str) {
+    let o = ast(name, text);
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Complete, "{text}");
+    let hit = o
+        .detections()
+        .iter()
+        .find(|d| d.rule_id == "TS_JWT_RSA_ALGS")
+        .expect("original algorithm reference retained");
+    let pos = text.find("RS256").unwrap();
+    let prefix = &text[..pos];
+    assert_eq!(
+        hit.location.line,
+        prefix.bytes().filter(|b| *b == b'\n').count() + 1
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        hit.location.column,
+        prefix.rsplit('\n').next().unwrap().chars().count() + 1
+    );
+    assert!(hit.evidence.snippet_preview.contains("RS256"));
+}
+#[test]
+fn empty_type_alias_parameters_are_accepted_without_accepting_empty_function_parameters() {
+    for prefix in [
+        "export type Shape<> = { ok: true };",
+        "type Shape< /* intentionally empty */ > = number;",
+        "type Shape<\n> = string;",
+    ] {
+        complete_with_original_hit("library.ts", &format!("{prefix}\nconst alg = 'RS256';"));
+    }
+    for source in [
+        "function f<>() {}",
+        "class C<> {}",
+        "type Shape<T = > = number;",
+        "type Shape<> = ;",
+    ] {
+        assert_eq!(
+            ast("library.ts", source).status(),
+            DetectorStatus::Partial,
+            "{source}"
+        );
+    }

+#[test]
+fn undeclared_platforms_bad_versions_and_bad_bundler_metadata_remain_incomplete() {
+    for spec in [
+        "1.2.3-unknown",
+        "1.2.3-alpha",
+        "1.2.3+garbage",
+        "1..2-arm64-darwin",
+    ] {
+        let text=format!("GEM\n  remote: https://rubygems.org/\n  specs:\n    openssl ({spec})\n\nPLATFORMS\n  arm64-darwin\n\nBUNDLED WITH\n  4.0.12\n");
+        assert_eq!(
+            dependency(&text).status(),
+            DetectorStatus::Partial,
+            "{spec}"
+        );
+    }
+    for metadata in ["  nonsense", "  4.0.12 extra"] {
+        let text=format!("GEM\n  remote: https://rubygems.org/\n  specs:\n    openssl (3.0.0)\n\nBUNDLED WITH\n{metadata}\n");
+        assert_eq!(dependency(&text).status(), DetectorStatus::Partial);
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn dockerfile_named_real_sources_are_still_parsed_as_their_languages() {
+    complete_with_original_hit(
+        "Dockerfile.java",
+        "public class Dockerfile { String alg = \"RS256\"; }",
+    );
+    complete_with_original_hit("Dockerfile.ts", "const alg = 'RS256';");
+    complete_with_original_hit("Dockerfile.py", "alg = 'RS256'");
+    let f = ScannableFile::from_bytes(
+        "Dockerfile.go".into(),
+        b"package image\nimport \"crypto/rsa\"\n".to_vec(),
+    );
+    assert_eq!(
+        TreeSitterDetector
+            .detect_detailed(&f, &rules())
+            .unwrap()
+            .status(),
+        DetectorStatus::Complete
+    );
+    assert!(RegexDetector
+        .detect(&f, &rules())
+        .unwrap()
+        .iter()
+        .any(|d| d.rule_id == "GO_TLS_RSA_IMPORT"));
+    for name in ["Dockerfile.go", "dockerfile.ts", "DOCKERFILE.JAVA"] {
+        let text="\u{feff}# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1\n\narg IMAGE=base\nfrom ${IMAGE}\nRUN echo 'import \"crypto/rsa\"'\nENV TOKEN_ALG RS256\n";
+        assert_eq!(ast(name, text).status(), DetectorStatus::NotApplicable);
+        let o = RegexDetector
+            .detect(
+                &ScannableFile::from_bytes(name.into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+                &rules(),
+            )
+            .unwrap();
+        assert!(o.iter().all(|d| !matches!(
+            d.rule_id.as_str(),
+            "GO_TLS_RSA_IMPORT" | "API_GO_CRYPTO_RSA"
+        )));
+        assert!(o.iter().any(|d| d.rule_id == "JWT_RS256"));
+    }
+}

+#[test]
+fn legacy_dsa_oid_requires_the_same_well_formed_public_parameters() {
+    use pqc_scan_detectors::certificate_detector::parse_public_key_der;
+    let params = der(0x30, &[2, 1, 23, 2, 1, 11, 2, 1, 2]);
+    for oid in [
+        &[0x2b, 0x0e, 3, 2, 12][..],
+        &[0x2a, 0x86, 0x48, 0xce, 0x38, 4, 1][..],
+    ] {
+        let key = parse_public_key_der(&spki(oid, &params, &[2, 1, 8], 0)).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(key.algorithm, "DSA");
+        assert_eq!(key.status, DetectorStatus::Complete);
+        for (p, y) in [
+            (&params[..], &[2, 1, 0][..]),
+            (&params[..], &[2, 1, 0x80][..]),
+            (&[0x30, 3, 2, 1, 23][..], &[2, 1, 8][..]),
+        ] {
+            assert_eq!(
+                parse_public_key_der(&spki(oid, p, y, 0)).unwrap().status,
+                DetectorStatus::Partial
+            );
+        }
+    }
