+use pqc_scan_core::{
+    scan_repository_native, Detector, DetectorStatus, NativeOptions, ScanConfig, ScannableFile,
+};
+use pqc_scan_detectors::{
+    dependency_detector::DependencyDetector, tree_sitter_detector::TreeSitterDetector,
+};
+use pqc_scan_rules::RuleSet;
+use std::{
+    path::{Path, PathBuf},
+    sync::Arc,
+};
+fn rules() -> RuleSet {
+    RuleSet::load_from_dir(&Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../rules")).unwrap()
+}
+fn ast(text: &str) -> pqc_scan_core::DetectorOutcome {
+    TreeSitterDetector
+        .detect_observed(
+            &ScannableFile::from_bytes("input.js".into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+            &rules(),
+        )
+        .unwrap()
+}
+#[test]
+fn intrinsic_prefixes_are_expressions_not_remainders_or_literal_text() {
+    for prefix in [
+        "if (%Check(x)) {}",
+        "while (%Check(x)) break;",
+        "for (; %Check(x);) break;",
+        "function g(){try { f(); } catch(e) {} %Check(x);}",
+        "const x = typeof %Check(y);",
+        "function f(){return %Check(x);}",
+    ] {
+        let source = format!("{prefix}\nconst alg = 'RS256';");
+        let o = ast(&source);
+        assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Complete, "{prefix}");
+        assert!(o
+            .detections()
+            .iter()
+            .any(|d| d.rule_id == "TS_JWT_RSA_ALGS" && d.location.line == 2));
+    }
+    for source in [
+        "const x = 1 % Check(y);",
+        "const s = '%Check(x)';",
+        "// %Check(x)\nconst x=1;",
+    ] {
+        assert_eq!(ast(source).status(), DetectorStatus::Complete, "{source}");
+    }
+    assert_eq!(
+        ast("const x = value %Check(y) unexpected;").status(),
+        DetectorStatus::Partial
+    );
+}
+#[test]
+fn sloppy_yield_and_runtime_negative_syntax_are_not_parse_errors() {
+    for source in [
+        "function f(yield) { yield() } f();",
+        "var yield; ({p: yield} = class {q=()=>42;});",
+        "try { parseInt() = 0; } catch(e) {}",
+        "for (f(x) in []) {}",
+        "assertThrows(()=>Array() **= 10, ReferenceError);",
+        "var x; [(x)] = [2]; [(x) = 3] = [];",
+        r"tag`\u``\01`();",
+    ] {
+        assert_eq!(ast(source).status(), DetectorStatus::Complete, "{source}");
+    }
+    for source in [
+        "'use strict'; function f(yield) {}",
+        "function yield(){'use strict';}",
+        "(function yield(){'use strict';})",
+        "function* gen(){'use strict'; function yield(){}}",
+        "function* f(){ yield = 1; }",
+        r"const bad = `\u`;",
+        "func_tion f(){}",
+        r"function* f(){yield`\u`;} bye();",
+        "'other directive'; 'use strict'; function f(yield){}",
+        r"async function f(){await `\u`;}",
+        r#"tag`x${ {a: 1} && "\u" }z`;"#,
+        r#"tag`x${ /}/.test(x) && "\u" }z`;"#,
+    ] {
+        assert_eq!(ast(source).status(), DetectorStatus::Partial, "{source}");
+    }
+}
+struct Fixture(PathBuf);
+impl Fixture {
+    fn new(tag: &str) -> Self {
+        let p = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("pqc-context-{}-{tag}", std::process::id()));
+        std::fs::create_dir_all(&p).unwrap();
+        Self(p)
+    }
+    fn put(&self, n: &str, s: &str) {
+        let p = self.0.join(n);
+        std::fs::create_dir_all(p.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
+        std::fs::write(p, s).unwrap();
+    }
+    fn scan(&self) -> serde_json::Value {
+        let c = ScanConfig {
+            root: self.0.clone(),
+            threads: 1,
+            ..Default::default()
+        };
+        let d: Vec<Arc<dyn Detector>> = vec![Arc::new(DependencyDetector)];
+        serde_json::to_value(
+            scan_repository_native(
+                &c,
+                &rules(),
+                &d,
+                &NativeOptions::new(Some("fixture")).unwrap(),
+            )
+            .unwrap()
+            .native()
+            .unwrap(),
+        )
+        .unwrap()
+    }
+}
+impl Drop for Fixture {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
+    }
+}
+#[test]
+fn local_parent_properties_are_expanded_without_registry_resolution() {
+    let f = Fixture::new("parent");
+    f.put("pom.xml","<project><groupId>org.example</groupId><artifactId>parent</artifactId><version>1</version><properties><crypto.version>1.2.3</crypto.version></properties></project>");
+    f.put("child/pom.xml","<project><parent><groupId>org.example</groupId><artifactId>parent</artifactId><version>1</version></parent><artifactId>child</artifactId><dependencies><dependency><groupId>org.example</groupId><artifactId>crypto</artifactId><version>${crypto.version}</version></dependency></dependencies></project>");
+    let n = f.scan();
+    assert_eq!(n["coverage"]["partial_files"], 0);
+    let a = n["assets"]
+        .as_array()
+        .unwrap()
+        .iter()
+        .find(|a| a["attributes"]["name"] == "org.example:crypto")
+        .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(a["attributes"]["declared_constraint"], "1.2.3");
+    assert!(a["attributes"]["resolved_version"].is_null());

+fn gemspec_static_dependency_declarations_have_parsed_provenance() {
+    let text="Gem::Specification.new do |s|\n s.name = 'example'\n s.add_dependency 'openssl', '~> 3.0'\nend\n";
+    let o = DependencyDetector
+        .detect_observed(
+            &ScannableFile::from_bytes("example.gemspec".into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+            &rules(),
+        )
+        .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Complete);
+    assert!(o.detections().iter().any(|d| d
+        .evidence
+        .metadata
+        .get("dep_name")
+        .is_some_and(|n| n == "openssl")
+        && d.location.line == 3));

+#[test]
+fn conditional_requirements_keep_both_declarations_and_do_not_resolve_either() {
+    let text =
+        "base==2.0\n{% if environment %}\nopenssl==3.0\n{% else %}\nopenssl==2.0\n{% endif %}\n";
+    let o = DependencyDetector
+        .detect_observed(
+            &ScannableFile::from_bytes("requirements.txt".into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+            &rules(),
+        )
+        .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Partial);
+    let hits: Vec<_> = o
+        .detections()
+        .iter()
+        .filter(|d| {
+            d.rule_id == "__DEPENDENCY_INVENTORY__" && d.evidence.metadata["dep_name"] == "openssl"
+        })
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(hits.len(), 2);
+    assert_eq!(hits[0].location.line, 3);
+    assert_eq!(hits[1].location.line, 5);
+    assert!(hits.iter().all(|d| d
+        .evidence
+        .metadata
+        .get("dep_context")
+        .is_some_and(|c| c == "conditional_declaration_not_effective")));
+}

+fn parent_scope_identity_conflicts_and_cycles_do_not_fabricate_facts() {
+    for (tag,parent,expected) in [
+  ("mismatch","<groupId>wrong</groupId><artifactId>parent</artifactId><version>1</version><properties><v>9.9</v></properties>",1),
+  ("conflict","<groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>parent</artifactId><version>1</version><properties><v>1</v><v>2</v></properties>",2),
+  ("idempotent","<groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>parent</artifactId><version>1</version><properties><v>1</v><v>1</v></properties>",0),
+ ] {
+  let f=Fixture::new(tag);f.put("pom.xml",&format!("<project>{parent}<dependencies><dependency><groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>root</artifactId><version>${{v}}</version></dependency></dependencies></project>"));
+  f.put("child/pom.xml","<project><parent><groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>parent</artifactId><version>1</version></parent><artifactId>child</artifactId><dependencies><dependency><groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>crypto</artifactId><version>${v}</version></dependency></dependencies></project>");
+  let n=f.scan();assert_eq!(n["coverage"]["partial_files"],expected,"{tag}");
+  if tag!="idempotent" {let a=n["assets"].as_array().unwrap().iter().find(|a|a["attributes"]["name"]=="g:crypto").unwrap();assert!(a["attributes"]["reported_version"].is_null(),"{tag}");}
+ }
+    let f = Fixture::new("parent-cycle");
+    for (name, other) in [("a", "b"), ("b", "a")] {
+        f.put(&format!("{name}/pom.xml"),&format!("<project><groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>{name}</artifactId><version>1</version><parent><groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>{other}</artifactId><version>1</version><relativePath>../{other}/pom.xml</relativePath></parent></project>"));
+    }
+    assert!(f.scan()["diagnostics"]
+        .as_array()
+        .unwrap()
+        .iter()
+        .any(|d| d["code"] == "detector_truncated"));
+}

+fn excluded_and_symlink_parents_are_not_read_for_context() {
+    for symlink in [false, true] {
+        let f = Fixture::new(if symlink {
+            "parent-link"
+        } else {
+            "parent-exclude"
+        });
+        f.put("real/pom.xml","<project><groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>parent</artifactId><version>1</version><properties><v>9.9.9</v></properties></project>");
+        if symlink {
+            std::fs::create_dir_all(f.0.join("parent")).unwrap();
+            std::os::unix::fs::symlink(f.0.join("real/pom.xml"), f.0.join("parent/pom.xml"))
+                .unwrap();
+        } else {
+            f.put(
+                "parent/pom.xml",
+                &std::fs::read_to_string(f.0.join("real/pom.xml")).unwrap(),
+            );
+            f.put(".gitignore", "/parent/\n");
+        }
+        f.put("child/pom.xml","<project><parent><groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>parent</artifactId><version>1</version><relativePath>../parent/pom.xml</relativePath></parent><artifactId>child</artifactId><dependencies><dependency><groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>crypto</artifactId><version>${v}</version></dependency></dependencies></project>");
+        let n = f.scan();
+        let a = n["assets"]
+            .as_array()
+            .unwrap()
+            .iter()
+            .find(|a| a["attributes"]["name"] == "g:crypto")
+            .unwrap();
+        assert!(a["attributes"]["reported_version"].is_null());
+        assert!(n["coverage"]["partial_files"].as_u64().unwrap() >= 1);
+    }
+}
+#[test]
+fn ruby_dynamic_or_invalid_declarations_stay_partial_and_strings_are_not_calls() {
+    for text in [
+        "gem ENV['NAME']",
+        "gem 'openssl', 'banana'",
+        "gem 'openssl', ENV['VERSION']",
+        "send(:gem, 'openssl')",
+    ] {
+        let o = DependencyDetector
+            .detect_observed(
+                &ScannableFile::from_bytes("Gemfile".into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+                &rules(),
+            )
+            .unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Partial, "{text}");
+    }
+    let o = DependencyDetector
+        .detect_observed(
+            &ScannableFile::from_bytes(
+                "Gemfile".into(),
+                b"# gem 'fake'\ntext=\"gem 'fake'\"\nif ENV['USE']\n gem 'openssl', '~> 3'\nend\n"
+                    .to_vec(),
+            ),
+            &rules(),
+        )
+        .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Complete);
+    assert!(o
+        .detections()
+        .iter()
+        .all(|d| d.evidence.metadata["dep_name"] == "openssl"));
+    assert!(o.detections().iter().any(|d| d
+        .evidence
+        .metadata
+        .get("dep_context")
+        .is_some_and(|s| s == "conditional_declaration_not_effective")));
+}

+#[test]
+fn delegated_gemspec_records_keep_their_own_file_and_rebound_receivers_are_not_trusted() {

+    f.put(
+        "x.gemspec",
+        "Gem::Specification.new do |s|\n s.name='x'\n s.add_dependency 'openssl', '~> 3.0'\nend\n",
+    );
+    let n = f.scan();
+    assert_eq!(n["coverage"]["partial_files"], 0);
+    let obs = n["observations"]
+        .as_array()
+        .unwrap()
+        .iter()
+        .find(|o| o["method"] == "dependency_report")
+        .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(obs["target"]["path"], "x.gemspec");
+    assert_eq!(obs["target"]["line"], 3);
+    let text = "Gem::Specification.new do |s|\ns = other\ns.add_dependency 'openssl', '1.0'\nend\n";
+    let o = DependencyDetector
+        .detect_observed(
+            &ScannableFile::from_bytes("x.gemspec".into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+            &rules(),
+        )
+        .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Partial);
+    assert!(o.detections().is_empty());

+        .iter()

+#[test]
+fn computed_fields_do_not_rewrite_nested_call_or_catch_parentheses() {
+    let source="function* maker(){ class C { #m(){} [(exported = function access(receiver,resume){try {receiver.#m();} catch(e) {} advance(resume);},yield 1,'x')] = 1; } return C; }\n%Check(maker);\nconst alg='RS256';";
+    assert_eq!(ast(source).status(), DetectorStatus::Complete);
+    assert_eq!(
+        ast("class C { [(function f(){advance value;},'x')]=1; }").status(),
+        DetectorStatus::Partial
+    );

+#[test]
+fn tagged_invalid_escapes_are_identified_despite_original_error_recovery() {
+    for source in [
+        r"tag`left${0}\u{}${1}right`;",
+        r"tag`\u{\u{0}${0}right`;",
+        r"tag`\u{-0}`; tag`\u{110000}`;",
+        r"tag`\xg${0}right`;",
+    ] {
+        assert_eq!(ast(source).status(), DetectorStatus::Complete, "{source}");
+    }
+    for source in [
+        r"if(x){} `\u{}`;",
+        r"if(x) `\u{}`;",
+        r"const x=`\u{}`;",
+        r"tag`ok${ '\u' }`;",
+    ] {
+        assert_eq!(ast(source).status(), DetectorStatus::Partial, "{source}");
+    }
+}

+#[test]
+fn javascript_unicode_whitespace_does_not_hide_intrinsic_expression_boundaries() {
+    for gap in ["\u{00a0}", "\u{feff}", "\u{2003}", "\u{2028}"] {
+        assert_eq!(
+            ast(&format!(
+                "const o={{valueOf:()=>{{{gap}%Check(x); return 20;}}}};"
+            ))
+            .status(),
+            DetectorStatus::Complete
+        );
+    }
+    assert_eq!(
+        ast("const x = value\u{00a0}%Check(y) unexpected;").status(),
+        DetectorStatus::Partial
+    );
+}

+        ("pom.xml","<project><modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion><properties><dep.version>1.2.3</dep.version></properties><dependencies><dependency><groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>a</artifactId><version>${dep.version}</version></dependency></dependencies></project>"),
+    ] { std::fs::write(f.0.join("input").join(name),source).unwrap(); assert_eq!(f.inspect(name,source).status(),DetectorStatus::Complete,"{name}"); }
+    let n = f.native();
+    let deps: Vec<_> = n["assets"]
+        .as_array()
+        .unwrap()
+        .iter()
+        .filter(|a| a["kind"] == "dependency")
+        .collect();
+    let attr = |name: &str| {
+        deps.iter()
+            .find(|a| a["attributes"]["name"] == name)
+            .unwrap()["attributes"]
+            .clone()
+    };
+    for (name, origin) in [
+        ("catalogpkg", "workspace_inherited"),
+        ("patchpkg", "source_unresolved"),
+        ("gitpkg", "git_dependency_unresolved"),
+    ] {
+        let a = attr(name);
+        assert_eq!(a["version_provenance"], origin);
+        assert!(a["resolved_version"].is_null());
+        assert!(a["declared_constraint"].is_null());
+        assert!(a["reported_version"].is_null());
+    }
+    let alias = deps
+        .iter()
+        .find(|a| {
+            a["attributes"]["name"] == "ring"
+                && a["attributes"]["version_provenance"] == "manifest_declared"
+        })
+        .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(alias["attributes"]["declared_constraint"], "^9.0.0");
+    assert!(alias["attributes"]["resolved_version"].is_null());
+    let locked = deps
+        .iter()
+        .find(|a| {
+            a["attributes"]["name"] == "ring"
+                && a["attributes"]["version_provenance"] == "lock_resolved"
+        })
+        .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(locked["attributes"]["resolved_version"], "9.0.2");
+    assert!(locked["attributes"]["declared_constraint"].is_null());
+    for (name, version, origin) in [
+        ("gitlib", "0.1.0", "git_dependency_unresolved"),
+        ("pathlib", "0.2.0", "path_dependency_unresolved"),
+    ] {
+        let a = attr(name);
+        assert_eq!(a["reported_version"], version);
+        assert_eq!(a["version_provenance"], origin);
+        assert!(a["resolved_version"].is_null());
+    }
+    assert_eq!(attr("openssl")["resolved_version"], "3.3.0");
+    for (name, version) in [("example.org/lib", "v1.2.3"), ("g:a", "1.2.3")] {
+        let a = attr(name);
+        assert_eq!(a["declared_constraint"], version);
+        assert_eq!(a["version_provenance"], "manifest_declared");
+        assert!(a["resolved_version"].is_null());
+    }
+    assert!(!deps
+        .iter()
+        .any(|a| a["attributes"]["resolved_version"] == "2.0.0"));
+    for properties in ["<a>${b}</a><b>${a}</b>", "<a>${missing}</a>"] {
+        let source=format!("<project><properties>{properties}</properties><dependencies><dependency><groupId>g</groupId><artifactId>a</artifactId><version>${{a}}</version></dependency></dependencies></project>");
+        assert_eq!(
+            f.inspect("pom.xml", &source).status(),
+            DetectorStatus::Partial
+        );
+    }
+}

+            .clone()

diff --git a/crates/pqc_scan_detectors/tests/inspection_repairs.rs b/crates/pqc_scan_detectors/tests/inspection_repairs.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..57668c9
--- /dev/null
+++ b/crates/pqc_scan_detectors/tests/inspection_repairs.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,537 @@
+use pqc_scan_core::{Detector, DetectorStatus, ScannableFile};
+use pqc_scan_detectors::{
+    dependency_detector::DependencyDetector, tree_sitter_detector::TreeSitterDetector,
+};
+use pqc_scan_rules::RuleSet;
+use std::{
+    path::PathBuf,
+    sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering},
+};
+struct Rules {
+    dir: PathBuf,
+    rules: RuleSet,
+}
+impl Rules {
+    fn new() -> Self {
+        static NEXT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
+        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
+            "pqc-inspection-repairs-{}-{}",
+            std::process::id(),
+            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
+        ));
+        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
+        std::fs::write(dir.join("rules.yml"),r#"[{"id":"TEST","kind":"tree_sitter","category":"test","severity":"high","risk":"quantum-vulnerable","confidence":0.9,"migration_hint":"Review","pattern":"RS256","scope":"javascript,typescript,python"}]"#).unwrap();
+        let rules = RuleSet::load_from_dir(&dir).unwrap();
+        Self { dir, rules }
+    }
+    fn inspect(&self, name: &str, text: &str) -> pqc_scan_core::DetectorOutcome {
+        TreeSitterDetector
+            .detect_detailed(
+                &ScannableFile::from_bytes(name.into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+                &self.rules,
+            )
+            .unwrap()
+    }
+}
+impl Drop for Rules {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.dir);
+    }
+}
+#[test]
+fn crypto_after_long_candidate_prefix_is_inspected() {
+    let r = Rules::new();
+    let text = format!("const value = '{} RS256';", "x".repeat(3000));
+    let o = r.inspect("long.js", &text);
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Complete);
+    assert_eq!(o.detections().len(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(
+        o.detections()[0].location.column,
+        text.find("RS256").unwrap() + 1
+    );
+}

+#[test]
+fn utf16_and_utf32_keep_findings_and_redact_decoded_source_snippets() {
+    let r = Rules::new();
+    let input = r.dir.join("input");
+    std::fs::create_dir_all(&input).unwrap();
+    let source = "const token = 'ENCODING_SYNTHETIC_CANARY'; // 日本😀\nuse('RS256');\n";
+    for (name, bytes) in [
+        (
+            "utf16le.js",
+            [
+                vec![0xff, 0xfe],
+                source.encode_utf16().flat_map(u16::to_le_bytes).collect(),
+            ]
+            .concat(),
+        ),
+        (
+            "utf16be.js",
+            [
+                vec![0xfe, 0xff],
+                source.encode_utf16().flat_map(u16::to_be_bytes).collect(),
+            ]
+            .concat(),
+        ),
+        (
+            "utf32le.js",
+            [
+                vec![0xff, 0xfe, 0, 0],
+                source
+                    .chars()
+                    .flat_map(|c| (c as u32).to_le_bytes())
+                    .collect(),
+            ]
+            .concat(),
+        ),
+        (
+            "utf32be.js",
+            [
+                vec![0, 0, 0xfe, 0xff],
+                source
+                    .chars()
+                    .flat_map(|c| (c as u32).to_be_bytes())
+                    .collect(),
+            ]
+            .concat(),
+        ),
+    ] {
+        std::fs::write(input.join(name), bytes).unwrap();
+    }
+    let config = pqc_scan_core::ScanConfig {
+        root: input,
+        threads: 1,
+        ..Default::default()
+    };
+    let d = pqc_scan_core::scan_repository_native(
+        &config,
+        &r.rules,
+        &pqc_scan_detectors::default_detectors(),
+        &pqc_scan_core::NativeOptions::new(Some("test/encoding")).unwrap(),
+    )
+    .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(d.coverage().analyzed_files(), 4);
+    assert_eq!(d.legacy().findings.len(), 4);
+    for f in &d.legacy().findings {
+        assert_eq!(f.location.line, 2);
+        assert_eq!(f.location.column, 6);
+        assert!(f.source_snippet.is_some());
+    }
+    let legacy = serde_json::to_string(d.legacy()).unwrap();
+    assert!(!legacy.contains("ENCODING_SYNTHETIC_CANARY"));
+    let native = serde_json::to_string(d.native().unwrap()).unwrap();
+    assert!(!native.contains("ENCODING_SYNTHETIC_CANARY"));
+    let native_json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&native).unwrap();
+    for o in native_json["observations"].as_array().unwrap() {
+        let path = o["target"]["path"].as_str().unwrap();
+        let (width, bom) = if path.starts_with("utf16") {
+            (2, 2)
+        } else {
+            (4, 4)
+        };
+        let prefix = &source[..source.find("RS256").unwrap()];
+        let offset = bom
+            + if width == 2 {
+                prefix.encode_utf16().count() * 2
+            } else {
+                prefix.chars().count() * 4
+            };
+        assert_eq!(o["target"]["byte_start"].as_u64(), Some(offset as u64));
+        assert_eq!(
+            o["target"]["byte_end"].as_u64(),
+            Some((offset + 5 * width) as u64)
+        );
+    }
+}
+#[test]
+fn malformed_wide_source_is_not_silently_excluded_or_counted_complete() {
+    let r = Rules::new();
+    let input = r.dir.join("input");
+    std::fs::create_dir_all(&input).unwrap();
+    std::fs::write(input.join("invalid.js"), [0xff, 0xfe, 0x00, 0xd8]).unwrap();
+    let d = pqc_scan_core::scan_repository_detailed(
+        &pqc_scan_core::ScanConfig {
+            root: input,
+            threads: 1,
+            ..Default::default()
+        },
+        &r.rules,
+        &pqc_scan_detectors::default_detectors(),
+    )
+    .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(d.coverage().selected_files(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(d.coverage().unsupported_files(), 1);
+    assert!(!d.coverage().is_complete());
+    assert!(d
+        .diagnostics()
+        .iter()
+        .any(|x| x.detector() == Some("text_decoder")));
+}
+#[test]
+fn file_selection_opt_out_retains_custom_inspection_and_crypto_inputs() {
+    let r = Rules::new();
+    let input = r.dir.join("input");
+    std::fs::create_dir_all(&input).unwrap();
+    for (name, bytes) in [
+        ("image.png", b"not actually an image".as_slice()),
+        ("code.js", b"use('RS256');".as_slice()),
+        ("module.wasm", b"\0asm\x01\0\0\0".as_slice()),
+        ("store.jks", b"\0\x01\0\xff".as_slice()),
+    ] {
+        std::fs::write(input.join(name), bytes).unwrap();
+    }
+    let config = pqc_scan_core::ScanConfig {
+        root: input,
+        threads: 1,
+        ..Default::default()
+    };
+    let detectors = pqc_scan_detectors::default_detectors();
+    let normal = pqc_scan_core::scan_repository_detailed(&config, &r.rules, &detectors).unwrap();
+    let all = pqc_scan_core::pipeline::scan_repository_with_selection(
+        &config,
+        &r.rules,
+        &detectors,
+        pqc_scan_core::FileSelection {
+            include_non_scannable: true,
+        },
+        None,
+    )
+    .unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(normal.coverage().selected_files(), 2);
+    assert_eq!(normal.coverage().excluded_enumerated_files(), 2);
+    assert_eq!(normal.coverage().not_applicable_files(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(all.coverage().selected_files(), 4);
+    assert_eq!(all.coverage().excluded_enumerated_files(), 0);
+    assert_eq!(all.coverage().not_applicable_files(), 3);
+    assert!(normal
+        .diagnostics()
+        .iter()
+        .all(|d| d.file() != Some("store.jks")));
+}

+#[test]
+fn encoded_pem_public_certificate_offsets_refer_to_original_bytes() {
+    let r = Rules::new();
+    let der = include_bytes!("fixtures/certificates/rsa2048.der");
+    let source = format!(
+        "\n{}",
+        pem::encode(&pem::Pem::new("CERTIFICATE", der.to_vec()))
+    );
+    // Use the repository's existing certificate rules; the fixture is public test data.
+    let rules =
+        RuleSet::load_from_dir(&PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../rules"))
+            .unwrap();
+    let mut utf8 = vec![0xef, 0xbb, 0xbf];
+    utf8.extend(source.as_bytes());
+    let cases = [
+        (utf8, 4),
+        (
+            [
+                vec![0xff, 0xfe],
+                source.encode_utf16().flat_map(u16::to_le_bytes).collect(),
+            ]
+            .concat(),
+            4,
+        ),
+        (
+            [
+                vec![0xfe, 0xff],
+                source.encode_utf16().flat_map(u16::to_be_bytes).collect(),
+            ]
+            .concat(),
+            4,
+        ),
+        (
+            [
+                vec![0xff, 0xfe, 0, 0],
+                source
+                    .chars()
+                    .flat_map(|c| (c as u32).to_le_bytes())
+                    .collect(),
+            ]
+            .concat(),
+            8,
+        ),
+        (
+            [
+                vec![0, 0, 0xfe, 0xff],
+                source
+                    .chars()
+                    .flat_map(|c| (c as u32).to_be_bytes())
+                    .collect(),
+            ]
+            .concat(),
+            8,
+        ),
+    ];
+    for (bytes, expected) in cases {
+        let file = ScannableFile::from_bytes(r.dir.join("certificate.pem"), bytes);
+        let o = pqc_scan_detectors::certificate_detector::CertificateDetector
+            .detect_detailed(&file, &rules)
+            .unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Complete);
+        let certs: Vec<_> = o
+            .detections()
+            .iter()
+            .filter(|d| d.evidence.metadata.contains_key("certificate_sha256"))
+            .collect();
+        assert!(!certs.is_empty());
+        for d in certs {
+            assert_eq!(d.location.line, 2);
+            assert_eq!(
+                d.evidence.metadata.get("byte_offset").map(String::as_str),
+                Some(expected.to_string().as_str())
+            );
+        }
+    }
+}

+fn repository_sized_dependency_values_are_supported_and_real_exhaustion_is_not_unsupported() {

+#[test]
+fn pnpm_boot_and_project_documents_are_both_inventoried_without_overwriting() {
+    let r = Rules::new();
+    let text="---\nlockfileVersion: '9.0'\npackages:\n  tool-only@1.0.0:\n    resolution: {integrity: sha512-test}\n---\nlockfileVersion: '9.0'\npackages:\n  app-only@2.0.0:\n    resolution: {integrity: sha512-test}\n";
+    let file = ScannableFile::from_bytes("pnpm-lock.yaml".into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec());
+    let o = DependencyDetector.detect_detailed(&file, &r.rules).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Complete);
+    assert_eq!(o.detections().len(), 2);
+    let names: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = o
+        .detections()
+        .iter()
+        .map(|d| d.evidence.metadata["dep_name"].as_str())
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(names, ["app-only", "tool-only"].into_iter().collect());
+    let too_many = format!("{text}---\nlockfileVersion: '9.0'\npackages: {{}}\n");
+    let file = ScannableFile::from_bytes("pnpm-lock.yaml".into(), too_many.into_bytes());
+    let o = DependencyDetector.detect_detailed(&file, &r.rules).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Unsupported);
+    let bad = format!("{text}broken: [\n");
+    let file = ScannableFile::from_bytes("pnpm-lock.yaml".into(), bad.into_bytes());
+    assert!(DependencyDetector.detect_detailed(&file, &r.rules).is_err());
+}

+        b"lockfileVersion: '9.0'\npackages: {}\nmetadata: |\n  --- &anchor *alias !tag\n".to_vec(),

+    let o = DependencyDetector.detect_detailed(&file, &r.rules).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Complete);

+#[test]
+fn pnpm_document_versions_and_shared_value_budget_are_enforced() {
+    let r = Rules::new();

+        ("lockfileVersion: 9", DetectorStatus::Complete),

+    ] {
+        let file = ScannableFile::from_bytes(
+            "pnpm-lock.yaml".into(),
+            format!("---\nlockfileVersion: '9.0'\npackages: {{}}\n---\n{second}\npackages: {{}}\n")
+                .into_bytes(),

+        let o = DependencyDetector.detect_detailed(&file, &r.rules).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(o.status(), expected);
+    }
+    let values = vec!["0"; 300_000].join(",");
+    let doc = format!("---\nlockfileVersion: '9.0'\npackages: {{}}\nmetadata: [{values}]\n");
+    let file =
+        ScannableFile::from_bytes("pnpm-lock.yaml".into(), format!("{doc}{doc}").into_bytes());
+    let o = DependencyDetector.detect_detailed(&file, &r.rules).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Truncated);

+#[test]
+fn duplicate_packages_in_two_documents_keep_distinct_native_record_ordinals() {
+    let r = Rules::new();
+    let input = r.dir.join("input");
+    std::fs::create_dir_all(&input).unwrap();
+    let doc="---\nlockfileVersion: '9.0'\npackages:\n  same@1.0.0:\n    resolution: {integrity: sha512-test}\n";
+    std::fs::write(input.join("pnpm-lock.yaml"), format!("{doc}{doc}")).unwrap();
+    let d = pqc_scan_core::scan_repository_native(
+        &pqc_scan_core::ScanConfig {
+            root: input,
+            threads: 1,
+            ..Default::default()
+        },
+        &r.rules,
+        &pqc_scan_detectors::default_detectors(),
+        &pqc_scan_core::NativeOptions::new(Some("test/pnpm-documents")).unwrap(),
+    )
+    .unwrap();
+    let native = serde_json::to_value(d.native().unwrap()).unwrap();
+    let observations = native["observations"].as_array().unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(observations.len(), 2);
+    let ordinals: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = observations
+        .iter()
+        .map(|o| o["target"]["object_index"].as_u64().unwrap())
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(ordinals, [1, 2].into_iter().collect());
+    assert_ne!(observations[0]["id"], observations[1]["id"]);
+}
+#[test]
+fn pnpm_record_limit_is_shared_by_both_documents_and_retains_partial_inventory() {
+    let r = Rules::new();
+    let doc = |start: usize, count: usize| {
+        let entries = (start..start + count)
+            .map(|i| format!("p{i}@1.0.0: {{resolution: {{integrity: sha512-test}}}} "))
+            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
+            .join(",");
+        format!("---\nlockfileVersion: '9.0'\npackages: {{{entries}}}\n")
+    };
+    for (extra, expected) in [
+        (0, DetectorStatus::Complete),
+        (1, DetectorStatus::Truncated),
+    ] {
+        let text = doc(0, 5000) + &doc(5000, 5000 + extra);
+        let file = ScannableFile::from_bytes("pnpm-lock.yaml".into(), text.into_bytes());
+        let o = DependencyDetector.detect_detailed(&file, &r.rules).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(o.status(), expected);
+        assert_eq!(o.detections().len(), 10_000);
+    }
+}

+use pqc_scan_core::{Detector, DetectorStatus, ScannableFile};
+use pqc_scan_detectors::{
+    dependency_detector::DependencyDetector, regex_detector::RegexDetector,
+    tree_sitter_detector::TreeSitterDetector,
+};
+use pqc_scan_rules::RuleSet;
+use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
+fn rules() -> RuleSet {
+    RuleSet::load_from_dir(&Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../rules")).unwrap()
+}
+fn ast(name: &str, text: &str) -> pqc_scan_core::DetectorOutcome {
+    TreeSitterDetector
+        .detect_detailed(
+            &ScannableFile::from_bytes(name.into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+            &rules(),
+        )
+        .unwrap()
+}
+fn has(name: &str, text: &str, id: &str) {
+    let o = ast(name, text);
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Complete, "{name}");
+    assert!(
+        o.detections().iter().any(|d| d.rule_id == id),
+        "missing {id} in {name}"
+    );
+}
+#[test]
+fn python_rsa_calls_and_aliases_are_detected() {

+fn symmetric_and_unrelated_calls_do_not_become_rsa() {

+            o.detections().iter().all(|d| ![

+                "TS_CLASSICAL_EC_API",
+                "TS_CLASSICAL_EDDSA_API"
+            ]
+            .contains(&d.rule_id.as_str())),
+            "{name}"
+        );
+    }

+#[test]
+fn go_paths_in_non_go_data_are_not_go_imports() {

+    for name in ["configdata.pm", "Makefile", "data.txt", "app.js"] {
+        let f = ScannableFile::from_bytes(name.into(), b"output/crypto/rsa/example.o".to_vec());
+        let o = RegexDetector.detect_detailed(&f, &r).unwrap();
+        assert!(o.detections().iter().all(|d| !matches!(
+            d.rule_id.as_str(),
+            "GO_TLS_RSA_IMPORT" | "API_GO_CRYPTO_RSA"
+        )));
+    }
+    let f = ScannableFile::from_bytes(
+        "a.go".into(),
+        b"package a\nimport \"crypto/rsa\"\n".to_vec(),
+    );
+    assert!(RegexDetector
+        .detect(&f, &r)
+        .unwrap()
+        .iter()
+        .any(|d| d.rule_id == "GO_TLS_RSA_IMPORT"));

+            "a.tsx",
+            "const x = <a href=\"https://example.invalid/?a=1&b=2\">Runtime & Build</a>;\n",

+        ("a.go", "package a\nvar p = new(int64(60))\n"),
+    ] {
+        let text = format!(
+            "{prefix}{}",
+            if name.ends_with(".go") {
+                "var alg = \"RS256\""
+            } else if name.ends_with(".rb") {
+                "alg = 'RS256'"
+            } else {
+                "const alg = 'RS256';"
+            }
+        );
+        let o = ast(name, &text);
+        assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Complete, "{name}");
+        assert!(o.detections().iter().any(
+            |d| d.rule_id == "TS_JWT_RSA_ALGS" && d.location.line == prefix.lines().count() + 1
+        ));
+    }

+fn dependency(name: &str, text: &str) -> pqc_scan_core::DetectorOutcome {
+    DependencyDetector
+        .detect_observed(
+            &ScannableFile::from_bytes(name.into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+            &rules(),
+        )
+        .unwrap()

+            assert!(d
+                .evidence
+                .metadata

+    }

+fn der(tag: u8, contents: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
+    let mut out = vec![tag];
+    if contents.len() < 128 {
+        out.push(contents.len() as u8);
+    } else {
+        let size = contents.len() as u16;
+        out.extend_from_slice(&[0x82, (size >> 8) as u8, size as u8]);
+    }
+    out.extend_from_slice(contents);
+    out
+}
+fn pqc_spki(last: u8, size: usize, parameters: &[u8], unused: u8) -> Vec<u8> {
+    let mut oid = vec![0x60, 0x86, 0x48, 1, 0x65, 3, 4, 3];
+    oid.push(last);
+    let mut alg = der(6, &oid);
+    alg.extend_from_slice(parameters);
+    let mut bits = vec![unused];
+    bits.extend(vec![7; size]);
+    let mut fields = der(0x30, &alg);
+    fields.extend(der(3, &bits));
+    der(0x30, &fields)
+}
+#[test]
+fn pqc_public_key_shapes_are_measured_without_rsa_bit_claims() {
+    use pqc_scan_detectors::certificate_detector::parse_public_key_der;
+    for (oid, size) in [
+        (17, 1312),
+        (18, 1952),
+        (19, 2592),
+        (20, 32),
+        (22, 48),
+        (24, 64),
+        (26, 32),
+        (28, 48),
+        (30, 64),
+    ] {
+        let a = parse_public_key_der(&pqc_spki(oid, size, &[], 0)).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(a.status, DetectorStatus::Complete, "oid {oid}");
+        assert_eq!(a.key_bits, None);
+        assert_eq!(a.encoded_bytes, size);
+        for invalid in [
+            pqc_spki(oid, size - 1, &[], 0),
+            pqc_spki(oid, size, &[5, 0], 0),
+            pqc_spki(oid, size, &[], 1),
+        ] {
+            assert!(parse_public_key_der(&invalid)
+                .map_or(true, |a| a.status != DetectorStatus::Complete));
+        }
+    }
+}
+#[test]
+fn non_certificate_pem_does_not_produce_unsupported_certificate_diagnostic() {

+    let f = ScannableFile::from_bytes(
+        "public.pem".into(),
+        b"-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----\nAA==\n-----END PUBLIC KEY-----\n".to_vec(),
+    );
+    let o = CertificateDetector.detect_observed(&f, &rules()).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::NotApplicable);
+    assert!(o.detections().is_empty());
+    assert_eq!(o.observations().len(), 1);
+}

+#[test]
+fn tls_versions_and_enum_symbols_are_not_misclassified_as_legacy_tls_or_suites() {
+    for text in [
+        "curl TLSv1_2",
+        "const options = { minVersion: 'TLSv1.3' };",

+    ] {
+        let o = RegexDetector
+            .detect(
+                &ScannableFile::from_bytes("a.js".into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),

+            o.iter().all(|d| ![
+                "TLS_VERSION_1_0",
+                "TLS_VERSION_1_1",
+                "NODE_TLS_MIN_VERSION",
+                "TLS_RSA_CIPHER_SUITE",
+                "TLS_RSA_KEY_EXCHANGE"
+            ]
+            .contains(&d.rule_id.as_str())),
+            "{text}"
+        );
+    }
+    for (text, id) in [
+        ("const version = 'TLSv1.0';", "TLS_VERSION_1_0"),
+        (
+            "const suite = 'TLS_RSA_WITH_AES_128_GCM_SHA256';",
+            "TLS_RSA_KEY_EXCHANGE",
+        ),
+    ] {
+        assert!(RegexDetector
+            .detect(
+                &ScannableFile::from_bytes("a.js".into(), text.as_bytes().to_vec()),
+                &rules()
+            )
+            .unwrap()
+            .iter()
+            .any(|d| d.rule_id == id));
+    }

+#[test]
+fn pqc_signature_oid_does_not_make_wrong_signature_shapes_complete() {
+    use pqc_scan_detectors::certificate_detector::parse_certificate_der;
+    let mut bytes = include_bytes!("fixtures/certificates/rsa2048.der").to_vec();
+    let old = [0x2a, 0x86, 0x48, 0x86, 0xf7, 0x0d, 1, 1, 11];
+    let new = [0x60, 0x86, 0x48, 1, 0x65, 3, 4, 3, 17];
+    let offsets: Vec<_> = bytes
+        .windows(old.len())
+        .enumerate()
+        .filter_map(|(i, b)| (b == old).then_some(i))
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(offsets.len(), 2);
+    for i in offsets {
+        bytes[i..i + 9].copy_from_slice(&new);
+    }
+    assert_ne!(
+        parse_certificate_der(&bytes).unwrap().status,
+        DetectorStatus::Complete
+    );
+}

+#[test]
+fn python_function_local_imports_do_not_bind_other_functions() {
+    let source="def one():\n    from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric import rsa as r\n\ndef two():\n    return r.generate_private_key(public_exponent=65537,key_size=4096)";

+        .detections()
+        .iter()

+    has("a.py", source, "TS_PY_RSA_USAGE");
+}

+fn der_fields(encoded: &[u8]) -> Vec<Vec<u8>> {
+    fn header(bytes: &[u8]) -> (usize, usize) {
+        if bytes[1] < 128 {
+            (2, usize::from(bytes[1]))
+        } else {
+            let n = usize::from(bytes[1] & 127);
+            let size = bytes[2..2 + n]
+                .iter()
+                .fold(0usize, |v, b| (v << 8) | usize::from(*b));
+            (2 + n, size)
+        }
+    }
+    let (h, n) = header(encoded);
+    let mut rest = &encoded[h..h + n];
+    let mut result = Vec::new();
+    while !rest.is_empty() {
+        let (h, n) = header(rest);
+        result.push(rest[..h + n].to_vec());
+        rest = &rest[h + n..];
+    }
+    result
+}
+fn pqc_signed_certificate(oid: u8, signature_size: usize, parameters: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
+    let mut fields = der_fields(include_bytes!("fixtures/certificates/rsa2048.der"));
+    let mut tbs = der_fields(&fields[0]);
+    let mut oid_bytes = vec![0x60, 0x86, 0x48, 1, 0x65, 3, 4, 3];
+    oid_bytes.push(oid);
+    let mut alg = der(6, &oid_bytes);
+    alg.extend(parameters);
+    let alg = der(0x30, &alg);
+    assert_eq!(tbs[0][0], 0xa0);
+    tbs[2] = alg.clone();
+    fields[0] = der(0x30, &tbs.concat());
+    fields[1] = alg;
+    let mut bits = vec![0];
+    bits.resize(signature_size + 1, 0x5a);
+    fields[2] = der(3, &bits);
+    der(0x30, &fields.concat())
+}
+#[test]
+fn pqc_certificate_signature_shapes_have_positive_and_adjacent_negative_controls() {
+    use pqc_scan_detectors::certificate_detector::parse_certificate_der;
+    // RFC9881 ML-DSA and RFC9909 SLH/HashSLH signature encodings; no trust validation claim.
+    for (oid, size) in [(17, 2420), (18, 3309), (19, 4627)]
+        .into_iter()
+        .chain((20..=43).map(|oid| {
+            (
+                oid,
+                [7856, 17088, 16224, 35664, 29792, 49856][usize::from((oid - 20) % 6)],
+            )
+        }))
+    {
+        assert_eq!(
+            parse_certificate_der(&pqc_signed_certificate(oid, size, &[]))
+                .unwrap()
+                .status,
+            DetectorStatus::Complete,
+            "{oid}"
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            parse_certificate_der(&pqc_signed_certificate(oid, size - 1, &[]))
+                .unwrap()
+                .status,
+            DetectorStatus::Partial,
+            "short {oid}"
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            parse_certificate_der(&pqc_signed_certificate(oid, size, &[5, 0]))
+                .unwrap()
+                .status,
+            DetectorStatus::Partial,
+            "params {oid}"
+        );
+    }
+}
+#[test]
+fn all_supported_pqc_public_key_oids_have_shape_controls() {
+    use pqc_scan_detectors::certificate_detector::parse_public_key_der;
+    for oid in 17..=31 {
+        let size = if oid < 20 {
+            [1312, 1952, 2592][usize::from(oid - 17)]
+        } else {
+            [32, 32, 48, 48, 64, 64][usize::from((oid - 20) % 6)]
+        };
+        let a = parse_public_key_der(&pqc_spki(oid, size, &[], 0)).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(a.status, DetectorStatus::Complete, "{oid}");
+        assert_eq!(a.key_bits, None);
+        assert_ne!(
+            parse_public_key_der(&pqc_spki(oid, size - 1, &[], 0))
+                .unwrap()
+                .status,
+            DetectorStatus::Complete,
+            "{oid}"
+        );
+    }
+}
+#[test]
+fn non_certificate_der_formats_remain_inapplicable_without_exposing_private_content() {
+    use pqc_scan_detectors::certificate_detector::CertificateDetector;
+    let rsa_public = der(0x30, &[der(2, &[17]), der(2, &[3])].concat());
+    let encrypted = der(
+        0x30,
+        &[
+            der(0x30, &der(6, &[0x2a, 3, 4])),
+            der(4, b"SECRET_DER_CANARY"),
+        ]
+        .concat(),
+    );
+    for bytes in [rsa_public, encrypted, pqc_spki(17, 1312, &[], 0)] {
+        let o = CertificateDetector
+            .detect_observed(
+                &ScannableFile::from_bytes("object.der".into(), bytes),
+                &rules(),
+            )
+            .unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::NotApplicable);
+        assert!(o.detections().is_empty());
+        assert!(!format!("{o:?}").contains("SECRET_DER_CANARY"));
+    }
+    for bytes in [
+        der(0x30, &[der(2, &[17]), der(2, &[3]), der(2, &[1])].concat()),
+        der(0x30, &der(4, b"not a certificate")),
+    ] {
+        let o = CertificateDetector
+            .detect_detailed(
+                &ScannableFile::from_bytes("bad.der".into(), bytes),
+                &rules(),
+            )
+            .unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(o.status(), DetectorStatus::Partial);
+    }
+}

+#[test]
+fn human_reports_summarize_exclusions_while_machine_diagnostics_keep_all_paths() {
+    let fixture = Fixture::new();
+    for i in 0..100 {
+        fs::write(
+            fixture.0.join(format!("input/picture-{i:03}.png")),
+            b"synthetic picture",
+        )
+        .unwrap();
+    }
+    let doc = fixture.document(DetectorStatus::Complete);
+    assert_eq!(doc.coverage().excluded_enumerated_files(), 100);
+    assert_eq!(doc.coverage().selected_files(), 1);
+    let output = fixture.0.join("reports");
+    write_document_reports(
+        &doc,
+        None,
+        true,
+        &output,
+        ReportFormat::All,
+        ReportLanguage::Ja,
+    )
+    .unwrap();
+    let html = fs::read_to_string(output.join("report.html")).unwrap();
+    let md = fs::read_to_string(output.join("report.md")).unwrap();
+    for content in [&html, &md] {
+        assert!(content.contains("100"));
+        assert!(content.contains("picture-000.png"));
+        assert!(content.contains("picture-002.png"));
+        assert!(!content.contains("picture-099.png"));
+    }
+    let diagnostic = serde_json::to_string(&doc.diagnostics_report()).unwrap();
+    assert!(diagnostic.contains("picture-099.png"));
+    assert_eq!(
+        doc.diagnostics()
+            .iter()
+            .filter(|d| d.code() == pqc_scan_core::DiagnosticCode::ExcludedPath)
+            .count(),
+        100
+    );
+}
